### [1] [Multi-Objective Molecular Generation with Frequency-Controlled Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27467)
*Elia Colleoni,Paolo Guida,Didier Barradas-Bautista,William Lafayette Roberts*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SpectralMol是一种基于进化计算和傅里叶系数的分子生成方法，通过NSGA-II算法实现多目标优化，无需预训练即可生成多样化的分子结构，在多项基准测试和真实药物发现任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于生成模型的分子生成方法需要大量预训练、存在训练数据统计偏差，且生成化学结构的可解释性有限。作者旨在提出一种无需预训练、可解释性强且高效的训练无关分子生成方法。

Method: 将化学结构表示为紧凑的傅里叶系数矩阵，投影到固定基上生成位置潜向量用于SELFIES解码；采用NSGA-II进化算法保证多样性并支持独立目标函数；通过低频和高频傅里叶模式的扰动分别实现骨架级修饰和局部子结构变异。

Result: 在标准化基准上达到可比整体性能，在多参数优化任务中表现最强；消融实验验证了结构化潜矩阵的优势；在ClpP靶向药物发现基准中，与强化学习方法相比，生成了更多对接命中数和更多样化的骨架，同时保持竞争性的理化性质。

Conclusion: 频率控制的进化动力学提供了一种可解释、高效且训练无关的多目标分子设计路径，傅里叶表示能够清晰分离骨架级修饰与局部子结构变异。

Abstract: Molecule generation methods that leverage generative models have been successfully applied to drug discovery. However, they often require extensive pre-training, suffer statistical biases in the training data, and might suffer from limited interpretability of generated chemical structures. In this work, we introduce SpectralMol, an algorithm based on evolutionary computation that processes chemical structures as a compact matrix of Fourier coefficients, projected onto a fixed basis to generate position-wise latent vectors for SELFIES decoding. The NSGA-II algorithm enforces diversity and enable separate objective functions rather than collapsed objectives into a scalar reward. The quality of the algorithm was tested against standardized benchmarks. The results show comparable aggregate benchmark performance with a task-dependent profile: SpectralMol is strongest on several multi-parameter optimization tasks. The same benchmark was used to perform an ablation study to demonstrate the advantages of a structured latent matrix. Finally, method was tested on a realistic ClpP-targeted drug-discovery benchmark, comparing it with the reinforcement-learning-based model under a fixed oracle-call budget. SpectralMol generates more docking hits and more diverse scaffolds while maintaining competitive physicochemical properties. The representation adopted in this work can cleanly separates scaffold-level modifications from localized substructure variations, as the former occur with perturbations of low-frequency Fourier modes and the latter with perturbations of high-frequency Fourier modes. The results support the evidence that frequency-controlled evolutionary dynamics provide an interpretable, efficient, and training-free route to multi-objective molecular design.

</details>


### [2] [DE-2LS: Differential Evolution with Late-Stage local-search for Unconstrained Single-Objective Numerical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27762)
*Dikshit Chauhan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-2LS，一个基于RDEx的晚期局部搜索增强差分进化框架，通过平滑的剥削偏置分支率更新和受保护的坐标模式局部搜索，在无约束单目标数值优化中平衡全局探索与局部开发。实验表明，DE-2LS相比原始RDEx在U-score上提升11.45%，相比其他CEC获奖算法提升34.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束单目标数值优化需要在全局探索、后期开发和函数评估效率之间取得谨慎平衡，现有方法在后期搜索阶段缺乏有效的局部精炼机制。

Method: DE-2LS保留了原始RDEx的进化搜索引擎，引入两个保守改进：1）在后期搜索阶段使用平滑的剥削偏置分支率更新（EB-rate smoothing）；2）引入受保护的坐标模式局部搜索作为预算感知的精炼机制。通过分阶段消融研究确定最终算法配置，使用基于U-score的评估联合反映解质量和收敛速度。

Result: DE-2LS在直接对比中持续优于原始RDEx，U-score从33602.0提升至37448.0（提升11.45%）；与多个CEC获奖算法相比，DE-2LS取得最佳整体U-score 178966.5，领先其他算法34.43%。

Conclusion: 精心设计的晚期局部搜索策略能够同时提高算法的收敛速度和最终解质量，验证了在差分进化后期引入局部搜索增强的有效性。

Abstract: Unconstrained single-objective numerical optimization requires a careful balance among global exploration, late-stage exploitation, and function-evaluation efficiency. This paper presents DE-2LS, a late-stage, local-search-enhanced differential evolution framework built on RDEx for unconstrained single-objective optimization with variable bounds. The proposed method preserves the original RDEx evolutionary search engine and introduces two conservative refinements: a smoothed exploitation-biased branch-rate update in the late search stage and a guarded coordinate-pattern local-search that serves as a budget-aware refinement mechanism. Since the considered setting is unconstrained apart from variable bounds, all selection and local-search acceptance decisions are based solely on objective values. To determine the final algorithm configuration, we conduct a staged ablation study by testing multiple settings of the EB-rate smoothing mechanism, the initial EB-rate, the standard-branch Gaussian sampling scale, the selection-pressure parameters, and the local-search coefficients. The final configuration is selected using a U-score-based evaluation that jointly reflects solution quality and convergence speed. Experimental results show that DE-2LS consistently improves the original RDEx in direct head-to-head comparison. In particular, DE-2LS increases the U-score from $33602.0$ to $37448.0$, corresponding to an improvement of $11.45\%$. Moreover, compared with several competitive and IEEE CEC-winning algorithms, DE-2LS achieves the best overall U-score of $178966.5$, outperforming the others by $34.43\%$. These results show that a carefully designed late-stage local-search strategy can improve both convergence speed and the final objective quality of the algorithm. The source code of DE-2LS is available at https://github.com/ChauhanDikshit?tab=repositories.

</details>


### [3] [DE-2LS: Differential Evolution with Lightweight Late Local Search for Constrained Numerical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27764)
*Dikshit Chauhan,Anupam Trivedi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-2LS，一种在RDEx框架基础上添加轻量级坐标模式局部搜索的差分进化变体，仅在后期激活以优化可行性、目标收敛和计算效率的平衡，在U-score上比RDEx提升5.58%，总排名在四种算法中最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定函数评估预算下，约束单目标数值优化需要在可行性、目标收敛和计算效率之间仔细平衡，现有方法在后期开发能力上仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于RDEx框架，保留其变异、交叉算子、成功历史自适应、存档机制、种群大小缩减和ε约束处理等组件；额外添加一个轻量级坐标模式局部搜索作为保护性精炼组件，仅在当前最优解附近运行，仅在运行后期激活，使用少量评估预算，并通过考虑可行性的比较规则接受候选解。

Result: 消融实验确认最终配置的U-score最优；与RDEx相比U-score提升5.58%；在四种算法对比中总U-score为80968，总排名为48，均优于RDEx、CL-SRDE和UDE-III。

Conclusion: 受控的后期精炼比激进或过早的局部搜索更有效，DE-2LS在保持RDEx速度优势的同时增强了其开发能力。

Abstract: Constrained single-objective numerical optimization requires a careful balance among feasibility, objective convergence, and computational efficiency under a fixed function-evaluation budget. This paper proposes DE-2LS, a late-stage, locally search-enhanced variant of differential evolution built on the RDEx framework. The proposed method preserves the original RDEx components, including mutation and crossover operators, success-history adaptation, archive mechanism, population-size reduction, and $ε$-based constraint handling. A lightweight coordinate-pattern local search is added as a guarded polishing component around the current best solution. It is activated only in the late stage of the run, uses a small evaluation budget, and accepts candidates through a feasibility-aware comparison rule. Ablation results show that the finalized DE-2LS configuration achieves the best U-score among all tested variants, confirming that controlled late-stage refinement is more effective than aggressive or premature local search. In the direct comparison with RDEx, DE-2LS achieves a 5.58\% gain in U-score. In the four-algorithm comparison, DE-2LS obtains the highest overall U-score of 80968 and the best total rank of 48 among RDEx, CL-SRDE, and UDE-III. These results indicate that DE-2LS improves the exploitation capability of the RDEx-based search framework while preserving its speed advantage under the combined speed-accuracy scoring criterion. The source code of DE-2LS is available at https://github.com/ChauhanDikshit?tab=repositories.

</details>


### [4] [MMAO: A Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer with Endogenous Resource Allocation for Continuous and Discrete Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28109)
*Jinliang Xu,Liping Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出代谢多智能体优化器（MMAO），通过私有一公共代谢资源循环实现内源性自适应，无需外部参数控制模块。在连续和离散优化场景中，同一控制律通过能量调节的感知、搜索幅度、角色漂移等机制实例化。实验表明MMAO是一种参数轻量、自校准的优化框架，其主要创新在于代谢驱动的异构搜索行为资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元启发式算法依赖固定种群大小、人工选择搜索尺度和外部参数控制模块，缺乏内源性自适应机制，参数调节成本高且跨领域泛化能力有限。

Method: MMAO构建私有一公共代谢资源循环：每个智能体携带内部能量、连续角色状态、运动/结构记忆和局部搜索历史，共享公共资源池。适应度改进通过鲁棒进步尺度和近期成功统计量归一化为代谢增益，闭环调控感知强度、搜索幅度、角色漂移、分支、剪枝、重生和精英再投资。连续域采用能量调节对称零阶探测和角色插值运动；离散域通过结构感知、局部路径改进、引导扰动和能量加权边重用实例化相同控制律。

Result: 在CEC2017子集（10D/30D，20个随机种子）和5个TSPLIB实例（100次离散运行）上进行可重复实验。证据支持MMAO主要作为参数轻量、自校准的优化框架，而非普遍优越的优化器。

Conclusion: MMAO的已验证原创性在于代谢内源性资源分配，能够协调异构搜索行为，为跨领域优化提供了一种自适应的、减少参数依赖的新范式。

Abstract: Traditional meta-heuristics often rely on fixed population sizes, manually chosen search scales, and externally attached parameter-control modules. This paper presents the \textit{Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer} (MMAO), a cross-domain optimization framework in which adaptation is derived endogenously from a private-public metabolic resource loop. Each agent carries internal energy, a continuous role state, motion or structural memory, and local search history, while the population shares a communal resource pool. Fitness improvements are converted into normalized metabolic gains through a robust progress scale and a recent success statistic; the same closed loop then regulates sensing intensity, search amplitude, role drift, branching, pruning, respawning, and elite reinvestment. In the continuous setting, MMAO uses energy-regulated symmetric zero-order probing and role-interpolated motion. In the discrete setting, the same control law is instantiated through structural sensing, local route improvement, guided perturbation, and energy-weighted edge reuse. The paper combines an implementation-faithful formulation with a reproducible experimental study on a CEC2017 subset (10D/30D, 20 seeds) and five TSPLIB instances (100 discrete runs in total). The current evidence supports MMAO primarily as a parameter-light, self-calibrating optimization framework whose main validated originality lies in metabolically endogenous resource allocation across heterogeneous search behaviors, rather than as a universally superior optimizer.

</details>

### [5] [Test-Input Generation for Tensor Programs: What Actually Finds Kernel Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27396)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对张量核测试输入生成这一经验性领域，系统评估了7种测试策略在26个算子集合（含16个正确实现和10个人工注入的LLM式错误）上的表现。核心发现：仅边界形状采样在召回率（78%）和假阳性率（0%）之间取得最佳平衡，而对抗性值采样虽召回率更高（99%）但假阳性率高达94%。边界策略对softmax尾部掩码bug的检测能力尤为突出（从0%提升至100%/62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量核的测试输入生成长期依赖经验性做法（如选取代表性形状和数据类型、固定形状的allclose检查），缺乏系统性的度量和对比。本文旨在将这些隐式选择显式化，并通过量化实验评估不同策略的优劣。

Method: 利用gpuemu操作模式感知的种子化模糊测试工具，在RTX 3060 GPU上对26个算子（16个正确控制+10个基于文档化转录模式注入的LLM风格错误）执行7种测试策略，策略在形状候选集、数据类型组合和输入值分布三个维度上变化，从bug召回率和控制假阳性率两个指标评估。

Result: 边界形状采样以78%召回率和0%假阳性率胜出；对抗性值采样召回率99%但假阳性率高达94%（因NaN/Inf注入触发验证器NaN检查）；常规策略对softmax尾部掩码bug的召回率为0%，边界策略提升至100%和62%。

Conclusion: 边界形状采样是操作上安全且有效的张量核测试策略；对抗性值采样需配合更精细的验证器设计才能降低假阳性；实验结果是关于各策略对不同注入错误模式的检测能力，而非特定部署LLM的错误率。

Abstract: Test-input generation for tensor kernels is folkloric. Most projects pick a representative shape and dtype, run a fixed-shape allclose-style check, and ship. We make the choices explicit and measure them. Using the gpuemu op-schema-aware seeded fuzzer (arXiv:2606.20128), we evaluate seven test-generation strategies across a 26-op corpus (16 correct controls and 10 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription patterns) on an RTX 3060 GPU instance. Strategies vary the shape candidate set, the dtype mix, and the input value distribution. We report each strategy on two axes: bug recall and control false-positive (FP) rate. Boundary-only shape sampling is the operationally safe winner: 78% recall on the 10 buggy kernels with 0% FP on the 16 controls. Adversarial value sampling reaches higher recall (99%) but inflates control FP to 94% because the strategy injects NaN and Inf inputs and the validator's NaN check fires on every kernel that propagates them, not only on buggy kernels. On the two softmax tail-mask bugs the "regular" strategy (no boundary shapes) catches 0%, while boundary raises recall to 100% and 62% respectively. That gap is the clearest single signal in the data. The corpus result is about which seeded bug patterns each strategy catches, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM.

</details>


### [6] [Implementing GenAI-Supported Learning in Software Engineering and Computer Science Education using Bloom's Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27398)
*Vahid Garousi,Zafar Jafarov,Aytan Mövsümova,Leyla Memmedova,Hüseyn Mirzayev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于布鲁姆认知分类法的生成式AI使用框架，通过在多个SE/CS课程中嵌入明确的教学指导，发现该框架能引导学生反思性地使用GenAI，尤其在高阶认知活动中价值显著，并提供了一种可扩展的、教学驱动的负责任GenAI应用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程教育中的应用带来了学习支持和学术诚信的双重挑战，现有研究缺乏如何通过明确的教学指导来引导负责任的GenAI使用的系统性方案。

Method: 设计了布鲁姆分类学对齐的GenAI框架，在不同认知水平上明确GenAI的角色；将该框架嵌入北爱尔兰女王大学和阿塞拜疆技术大学多个SE/CS课程的课程说明、实验和评估中；通过匿名问卷和学习制品收集数据，使用主题分析法并以布鲁姆分类学作为分析透镜。

Result: 学生认为GenAI在高阶认知活动（分析、评价、反思）中最有价值，而在基础学习中适用性较低。明确的布鲁姆层级指导促使学生反思性地使用GenAI，在需要独立思考时主动延迟或有意不使用。师生均报告了教学效益，同时也面临认知努力和教学设计工作量的挑战。

Conclusion: GenAI的教育价值在于认知学习目标、教学指导和学习者自我调节之间的有意识对齐。布鲁姆分类学为SE/CS教育中负责任的GenAI使用提供了可扩展的、教学驱动的框架，是强制监管式应对方法之外的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI adoption in software engineering education raises opportunities for learning support alongside concerns about superficial learning and academic integrity.
  Objective: This study investigates how explicit instructional guidance aligned with Bloom's taxonomy supports responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, exploring student and instructor perceptions of GenAI-supported learning.
  Method: We designed a Bloom-aligned GenAI framework that articulated appropriate GenAI roles at different cognitive levels. The framework was embedded in course instructions, labs, and assessment across multiple SE/CS courses at Queen's University Belfast and Azerbaijan Technical University. Data were collected via anonymous questionnaires and learning artifacts, analyzed using thematic analysis with Bloom's taxonomy as an analytic lens.
  Results: Students perceived GenAI as most valuable for higher-order cognitive activities (analysis, evaluation, reflection) and less suitable for foundational learning. Explicit Bloom-level guidance influenced students to use GenAI reflectively, with delayed or intentional non-use when independent thinking was prioritized. Both students and instructors reported pedagogical benefits alongside challenges in cognitive effort and instructional design workload.
  Conclusion: GenAI's educational value lies in intentional alignment between cognitive learning goals, instructional guidance, and learner self-regulation. Bloom's taxonomy provides a scalable, pedagogy-driven framework for responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, offering a practical alternative to enforcement-focused responses.

</details>


### [7] [Formal Grammars in Business Process Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27399)
*Milliam Maxime Zekeng Ndadji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了34篇关于形式文法与业务流程管理交叉研究的主要文献，识别出七个研究流，揭示了形式文法在BPM全生命周期中的影响，并指出各流之间缺乏综合，提出了五个开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式文法为BPM提供了生成、声明和分析能力，但相关研究分散于七个独立流中，缺乏系统性梳理与跨流综合，亟需通过文献综述明确现状、识别差距并推动统一发展。

Method: 对34篇主要研究进行系统性文献综述，识别并分类七个研究流，综合每个流的贡献、使用的形式化方法和局限性。

Result: 识别出七个研究流：(i) 流程语法用于组织流程设计；(ii) Bunge-Wand-Weber本体框架下评估流程建模语言为语法；(iii) 产生式规则语法用于流程结构规范和变体管理；(iv) 属性语法用于工作流的声明式规范和分布式执行；(v) 图语法用于流程模型的转换、生成和语义分析；(vi) 语法推断用于流程挖掘和发现；(vii) 流程代数作为类似语法的组合框架用于行为规范和验证。

Conclusion: 形式文法已影响BPM全生命周期，但七个研究流相对独立发展，缺乏交叉综合。更深层次、统一地利用语法理论有望推动BPM领域的发展。

Abstract: Business Process Management (BPM) is concerned with the systematic design, execution, monitoring, and improvement of business processes. Formal grammars have emerged as a particularly fruitful formalism for BPM, offering generative, declarative, and analytical capabilities that are uniquely well-suited to process-oriented concerns. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 34 primary studies at the intersection of formal grammars and BPM. We identify seven research streams: (i) process grammars for organizational process design; (ii) process modeling languages evaluated as grammars under the Bunge-Wand-Weber ontological framework; (iii) production-rule grammars for process structural specification and variant management; (iv) attribute grammars for the declarative specification and distributed execution of workflows; (v) graph grammars for the transformation, generation, and semantic analysis of process models; (vi) grammatical inference for process mining and discovery; and (vii) process algebras as grammar-like compositional frameworks for behavioral specification and verification. For each stream, we synthesize contributions, formalisms employed, and limitations. The review reveals that formal grammars have influenced BPM across every lifecycle phase (from organizational design to formal verification and data-driven discovery) yet the seven streams have developed largely in parallel, without cross-stream synthesis. We identify five corpus-grounded open challenges and argue that a deeper, unified exploitation of grammatical theory holds significant promise for advancing the state of the art in BPM.

</details>


### [8] [Recall Before Rerank: Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Large-Scale Code-to-Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27401)
*Leonardo Venuta,Francesco Tosoni,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了当代深度学习模型在大规模代码搜索中第一阶段的召回效果，发现现有模型在TB级源码集合上存在精度和可扩展性瓶颈。基于LLM的代码归一化和查询重写方案可提升低性能模型的精度，但整体上对资源受限部署的可持续性和代码专用LLM的鲁棒性提出了质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义代码搜索和克隆检测对软件开发、维护和复用至关重要，而当前深度学习模型在大规模code-to-code搜索引擎中的实际表现缺乏系统性评估，尤其是在TB级源码集合上的精度和可扩展性边界尚不明确。

Method: 在多编程语言、多数据集上对当代深度学习模型进行基准测试，评估其在code-to-code搜索第一阶段召回中的有效性、效率和可扩展性；并提出基于LLM的代码归一化（code normalisation）和查询重写（query-rewriting）方案。

Result: 现有模型在TB级源码集合上存在精度和可扩展性的关键限制；LLM-based代码归一化和查询重写对低性能模型有显著精度提升；对资源受限部署的可持续性和代码专用LLM的跨数据集鲁棒性提出质疑。

Conclusion: 为构建可扩展、高效的代码检索系统提供了可操作的洞见，并指出当前代码专用LLM在跨数据集场景下的鲁棒性不足，资源受限部署面临可持续性挑战。

Abstract: Semantic code search and clone detection are essential for software development, maintenance, and reuse. This paper evaluates the effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability of contemporary deep learning models for first-stage recall in large-scale code-to-code search engines. Benchmarking across multiple programming languages and datasets reveals critical limits in the precision and scalability of these models on Terabyte-scale source-code collections. We present LLM-based code normalisation and query-rewriting schemes that yield significant gains in precision for lower-performing models. Our results question the sustainability of resource-constrained deployment and the assumed robustness of current code-specialised LLMs across datasets. We conclude with actionable insights for building scalable, efficient code-retrieval systems.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Evaluation of Implicit Software World Models in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27406)
*Egor Bogomolov,Yaroslav Zharov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出从执行资源维度（峰值内存、耗时、分析器输出）评估AI模型对软件执行过程的理解，发现前沿模型表现欠佳，暗示模型缺乏对软件执行机制的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码执行基准仅关注控制流这一单一维度，作者希望将评估扩展到执行资源维度，以更全面地衡量模型对软件执行过程的理解。

Method: 以SWE-bench Verified为数据源，让模型预测测试结果、异常类别、峰值内存、墙钟时间以及方法和行粒度的分析器输出排名。

Result: 所有测试模型（包括前沿模型）表现均不理想，且行为脆弱不稳定。

Conclusion: 当前模型对软件如何执行缺乏显著理解，仅擅长编写源代码而非理解执行过程。

Abstract: Software engineering, whether performed by humans or by AI agents, requires reasoning about how software behaves. We call the internal model that supports such reasoning the software world model, and view current code-execution benchmarks as covering one well-studied slice of it -- control flow. In this paper, we take a step toward a broader evaluation by shifting the observable axis to execution resources: alongside test outcome and exception class, we predict peak memory, wall-clock time, and ranked profiler outputs at method and line granularity. We use SWE-bench Verified as the source of data to hold the test close to real-world software engineering tasks. All tested models, frontier ones included, show modest performance and brittle behaviour, suggesting a notable lack of understanding of how software is executed, as opposed to how its source code is written.

</details>


### [10] [Speculative Refinement: A Hybrid Autoregressive Diffusion Decoding Strategy and Its Behavior Across Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27474)
*Aditi Gupta,Neel Mishra,Kushagra Trivedi,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过SpecRef（一种无需训练的AR+扩散混合解码方法）研究混合生成系统的评估问题，发现了代码基准测试混淆结构发现与逻辑正确性、多阶段校正的"精炼张力"现象、对数似然与生成评估产生不同模型排名、以及标准后处理对非AR生成器失效等关键问题，为多阶段生成系统的诊断性评估提供了重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估协议无法有效衡量结合了自回归解码与扩散解码的混合生成系统，需要系统性地揭示评估方法中的潜在偏差和局限性。

Method: 以SpecRef（基于熵引导选择性掩码的从AR草稿热启动掩码扩散语言模型的无训练混合方法）为研究载体，在HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K、BBH、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag六个基准上采用执行式pass@1、精确匹配、对数似然评分三种评估协议进行系统分析。

Result: (1)代码基准中提供语法脚手架可将准确率从接近零提升至20%以上；(2)发现"精炼张力"现象——多阶段校正会破坏已正确生成的token；(3)对数似然与生成式评估对同一模型对产生不同排序；(4)标准Python后处理会静默破坏非AR生成器的代码评估。

Conclusion: 这些发现适用于任何多阶段或非自回归生成流水线，应转向更具诊断性的评估实践。

Abstract: How should we evaluate generation systems that combine autoregressive (AR) and diffusion decoding? We study this question through Speculative Refinement (SpecRef), a training-free hybrid method that warm-starts a masked diffusion language model from an AR draft using entropy-guided selective masking. Evaluating SpecRef across six benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, GSM8K, BBH, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag) with three distinct evaluation protocols (execution-based pass@1, exact-match, log-likelihood scoring), we surface several findings relevant beyond our specific system: (1) code benchmarks conflate structural discovery with logical correctness: providing a syntactic scaffold lifts accuracy from near zero to over 20% without changing the model, indicating that much of the baseline failure is structural; (2) a refinement tension phenomenon where multi-stage correction degrades already-correct tokens, exposing benchmark saturation ceilings invisible to single-model evaluation; (3) log-likelihood and generative evaluation produce different model rankings for the same model pair, suggesting they measure different capabilities; (4) standard Python post-processing silently breaks code evaluation for non-AR generators. These observations apply to any multi-stage or non-autoregressive generation pipeline and point toward more diagnostic evaluation practices.

</details>


### [11] [Contextual Associations Between Webpage Elements for Web Accessibility: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27506)
*Kishan Rakesh,Shiyi Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查能否从无障碍树中通过链接预测恢复网页元素间的人类感知上下文关联，并验证所学关联是否跨网站泛化。方法是将35个网站的无障碍树构建为图，结合DOM/CSS的空间与语义特征，比较四种机器学习模型与两种启发式基线。初步试点实验显示Hit@10范围为0.16–0.85（学习模型）和0.08–0.30（基线）。最终结果待完整实验后报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕阅读器用户通过元素列表导航网页时，常遇到如"Read more"这类符合W3C规范但在孤立语境下无法理解的 accessible names。页面中存在能使这些名称有意义的周围元素，但没有任何机制将这些元素与目标关联。尚无前人工作实证研究如何选择与给定目标上下文相关的周围元素。

Method: 构建基于35个网站（按Tranco top百万列表分层）的人工标注上下文关联数据集，每页由三位独立标注者标注。将每个页面从其无障碍树派生出图结构，结合DOM和CSS中的空间与语义特征。在留一站点交叉验证下，比较四种机器学习模型（MLP、GCN、GAT、SEAL）与两种启发式基线，使用Hit@K和MRR指标，采用预注册的统计框架。

Result: 已进行五个站点的作者标注试点研究，用于建立流程和参数化功效模拟。试点实验中，四种学习模型的Hit@10范围为0.16–0.85，两种启发式基线为0.08–0.30。最终结果将在计划实验和分析完成后报告。

Conclusion: 本研究的贡献包括：(1) 一个网页元素上下文关联的人工标注数据集；(2) 对无障碍树图上使用链接预测进行上下文选择的实证评估；(3) 跨站点泛化分析。

Abstract: [Context] Screen reader users navigating webpages by element list often encounter accessible names such as "Read more" that are valid under the W3C Accessible Name and Description Computation specification but uninterpretable in isolation. The surrounding elements that would make these names meaningful exist in the page but are not linked to the target by any mechanism. No prior work has empirically studied how to select which surrounding elements are contextually relevant to a given target. [Objective] This registered report investigates whether human-perceived contextual associations between webpage elements can be recovered from the accessibility tree using link prediction, and whether the learned associations generalize across websites. [Method] We will construct a dataset of human-annotated contextual associations on 35 websites, stratified across the Tranco top-million list, with three independent annotators per page. Each page is represented as a graph derived from its accessibility tree, augmented with spatial and semantic features from the DOM and CSS. We compare four machine learning models (MLP, GCN, GAT, and SEAL) against two heuristic baselines under leave-one-site-out cross-validation with a pre-registered statistical framework, using Hit@K and MRR. [Results] We have conducted a five-site author-annotated pilot study to establish the pipelines and parameterize the power simulation, with pilot Hit@10 ranging from 0.16 to 0.85 across four learned models and 0.08 to 0.30 across two heuristic baselines. The final results will be reported after the planned experiments and analyses are completed. [Conclusion] The study contributes a human-annotated dataset of contextual associations on webpages, an empirical evaluation of link prediction for context selection on accessibility-tree graphs, and a cross-site generalization analysis.

</details>


### [12] [Test Case Selection for Deep Neural Networks: A Replication Study on LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27601)
*Ali Asgari,Mitchell Olsthoorn,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对深度神经网络测试用例选择（TCS）技术在LLM代码模型上的有效性进行了大规模重复性研究，发现仅有部分视觉DNN领域的结论可泛化到代码LLM场景：基于不确定性的特征对早期故障发现有效，而基于表示的特征对准确性估计更稳健，且效果因任务和模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCS技术研究主要聚焦于视觉DNN，其在LLM代码模型上的有效性尚不明确，需要系统性验证其泛化能力。

Method: 在克隆检测、漏洞检测和技术债务预测三个代码分类任务上，对17个微调模型实例、7个预测特征和13种选择策略（含12种特征感知策略及随机采样基线）进行系统评估，从准确性估计和早期故障发现两个维度衡量性能。

Result: 1）仅有部分视觉DNN的发现可泛化到代码LLM场景；2）不确定性特征对早期故障发现有效，表示特征对准确性估计更鲁棒；3）TCS有效性因任务和模型而异，具有上下文依赖性。

Conclusion: 本研究为TCS技术超越视觉DNN的重复性提供了实证证据，并为代码LLM的运行评估提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Recently, test case selection (TCS) techniques have been explored to support the operational evaluation of deep neural networks (DNNs) under limited testing budgets, where labeling cost is a primary concern and uncovering model failures early is a key objective. Although prior studies report promising results, existing empirical evaluations focus almost exclusively on vision-based DNNs and datasets, leaving it unclear whether prior findings generalize to LLM code models. This paper presents a large-scale replication study of TCS techniques in the context of LLM code models. We re-examine established TCS strategies originally proposed for DNNs and complement them with statistical sampling strategies not previously evaluated for TCS. We assess their effectiveness on three code-related classification tasks: clone detection, vulnerability detection, and technical debt prediction. The study spans 17 task-specific fine-tuned model instances, 7 predictive features, and 13 selection strategies, including 12 feature-aware strategies and simple random sampling (SRS) as a feature-agnostic baseline. We evaluate performance along two dimensions: accuracy estimation and early failure discovery. The results indicate that only a subset of findings reported for vision-based DNNs generalize when TCS is applied to LLMs for code. In particular, uncertainty-based features are effective for early failure discovery, while representation-based features are more robust for accuracy estimation. At the same time, performance varies substantially across tasks and models, indicating that TCS effectiveness is context-dependent. Overall, this study provides empirical evidence on the replicability of TCS techniques beyond vision-based deep learning and offers insights into their use for the operational evaluation of LLMs for code.

</details>


### [13] [LLM-Assisted Model-Based GUI Testing for Vue.js Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27665)
*Tao Li,Chenhui Cui,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出LLMVue框架，利用大语言模型从Vue.js源码中生成可执行的页面过渡图(PTG)，实现基于模型的GUI测试。在10个开源项目上评估，使用GPT-4o构建的PTG具有高精度和召回率，低图编辑距离，相比随机探索基线显著提升覆盖率和探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vue.js作为流行的现代Web应用框架，其可靠性通过自动化测试保障日益重要。基于模型的GUI测试在其他平台上已成功应用，但在Vue.js上仍面临挑战：过渡候选（分布在路由配置和单文件组件中）需要具体化并归一化为可执行的页面过渡图(PTG)以进行测试。

Method: 提出LLMVue框架，使用大语言模型(LLM)从Vue.js源代码生成PTG。LLMVue推断组件层次结构和路由过渡，将其合并为跨多个SFC的统一PTG。使用GPT-4o作为LLM骨干，在10个开源Vue.js项目上评估。

Result: 构建的图具有高精度和召回率，低图编辑距离。LLMVue引导的测试在相同时间约束下，覆盖率和探索效率显著优于随机探索基线。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次使用LLM进行基于模型的Vue.js应用GUI测试，采用源码级PTG提取方法。

Abstract: Vue.js is a popular framework for building modern web applications. As Vue.js functionality and tooling support grow, ensuring its reliability (through automated testing) is becoming increasingly important. Although model-based testing has been successfully used to automate graphical user interface (GUI) testing on other platforms, its application to Vue.js remains challenging: Transition candidates, which are spread across router configurations and single-file components (SFCs), must be concretized and normalized into an executable page transition graph (PTG) for testing. To address this, we propose the LLMVue framework, which uses a large language model (LLM) to generate a PTG from Vue.js source code. LLMVue infers component hierarchies and route transitions, merging them into a unified PTG across multiple SFCs. We evaluated LLMVue on a collection of ten open-source Vue.js projects from GitHub, using GPT-4o as the LLM backbone. The constructed graphs demonstrate high precision and recall, with low graph edit distance. LLMVue -guided testing also significantly improves the coverage and exploration efficiency, compared to a random exploration baseline (with the same time constraints). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of LLMs for model-based GUI testing of Vue.js applications using source-level PTG extraction.

</details>


### [14] [BashCoder-R1: Towards Robust and Explainable Bash Code Generation with Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27733)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jia Xu,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jiajia Ma,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Zenghua Wang,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BashCoder-R1框架，通过持续预训练、长链思维监督微调和鲁棒性感知强化学习，在Bash脚本生成任务上显著超越DeepSeek-V3.2等基线模型，在语法正确性、鲁棒性和功能完整性上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在自动生成Bash脚本时存在两大问题：不可审计的"黑箱"推理过程，以及生成的代码存在关键鲁棒性漏洞。这两个问题相互关联，影响系统稳定性和安全性。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1) 持续预训练(CPT)使模型专业化于Bash范式；(2) 长链思维监督微调(L-CoT SFT)在专家验证的推理-代码样本上训练，模拟主动风险感知思维；(3) 鲁棒性感知组相对策略优化(R-GRPO)，通过加权奖励优化语法正确性、鲁棒性（shellcheck检测）和格式正确性。

Result: 在包含952个真实任务的BashBench基准上，单行/多行任务分别达到：SyntaxPass (100.00%/94.97%)、RobustWarnRate (4.01%/16.47%)、RobustPass (95.99%/79.33%)、FuncRate (93.01%/93.85%)、FullRate (90.04%/73.18%)，在FullRate上比最强基线DeepSeek-V3.2分别高出37.82%和20.18%。

Conclusion: BashCoder-R1通过结合CPT、L-CoT SFT和R-GRPO三阶段训练，有效解决了Bash脚本生成中的黑箱推理和鲁棒性漏洞问题，在功能、鲁棒性和清晰度方面均获得最高质量评分。

Abstract: Bash scripts are the cornerstone of system administration and DevOps automation, where code quality directly impacts system stability and security. In automated Bash script generation using Large Language Models (LLMs), two interconnected failures emerge: unauditable "black box" reasoning and critical robustness vulnerabilities in generated code. To address both, we propose BashCoder-R1, a novel framework for robust and explainable Bash script generation. Our pipeline combines: (1) Continual Pre-training (CPT) to specialize the model on Bash paradigms; (2) Long Chain-of-Thought Supervised Fine-Tuning (L-CoT SFT) on expert-validated reasoning-and-code samples to emulate proactive risk-aware thinking; and (3) Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (R-GRPO), a reinforcement learning phase optimizing a weighted reward for syntax correctness, robustness (via shellcheck), and format correctness. We evaluate on BashBench, a new benchmark of 952 real-world tasks (773 single-line, 179 multi-line). BashCoder-R1 achieves SyntaxPass (100.00%/94.97%), RobustWarnRate (4.01%/16.47%), RobustPass (95.99%/79.33%), FuncRate (93.01%/93.85%), and FullRate (90.04%/73.18%) for single-line/multi-line tasks, outperforming the strongest baseline DeepSeek-V3.2 (Reasoning) by 37.82% and 20.18% in FullRate. Human evaluation on Functionality, Robustness, and Clarity further confirms BashCoder-R1 achieves the highest quality ratings.

</details>


### [15] [UNICS: Multilingual Code Search via Unified Pseudocode and Contrastive Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27747)
*Ye Fan,Jidong Ge,Chuanyi Li,Liguo Huang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UNICS是一个两阶段训练的多语言代码搜索框架，第一阶段使用伪代码作为统一表示进行预训练以学习跨语言算法级逻辑，第二阶段通过多任务迁移学习（包括硬正例挖掘和跨语言动态硬负例采样）适应特定语言，在多个基准测试中达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练模型在多语言代码搜索中存在数据不平衡、跨语言语义干扰以及统一表示（如AST、IR）丢失关键信息的问题；同时传统对比学习策略仅依赖简单的硬负例采样，忽视了通过挖掘硬正例来学习代码内在语义不变性的潜力。

Method: 提出UNICS框架，采用两阶段训练策略。第一阶段：在构建的新数据集上使用伪代码作为统一表示进行预训练，学习保真的跨语言算法级逻辑。第二阶段：采用多任务迁移学习，将代码分解为语义切片（如API调用、函数体），并结合硬正例挖掘和跨语言动态硬负例采样任务，将通用知识适应到特定语言。

Result: 在多个多语言和跨语言代码搜索基准测试中达到最先进性能，特别是在低资源语言的零样本迁移任务中展现出优越的泛化能力和性能平衡。

Conclusion: UNICS通过伪代码统一表示和两阶段训练策略，有效解决了多语言代码搜索中的数据不平衡、语义干扰和信息丢失问题，同时利用硬正例挖掘和动态硬负例采样提升了对比学习的效果。

Abstract: While pre-trained models have achieved remarkable success in code search, their multilingual capabilities remain a major hurdle, plagued by data imbalance, cross-lingual semantic interference, and the loss of critical information from existing unified representations like Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) or Intermediate Representations (IRs). Furthermore, conventional contrastive learning strategies often rely on simplistic hard negative sampling while overlooking the potential of mining hard positives to learn code's intrinsic semantic invariance. To address these challenges, we introduce UNICS, a framework for multilingual code search built on a two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, UNICS is pre-trained on a novel dataset we constructed, which uses pseudo-code as a unified representation to learn a cross-lingual, algorithm-level logic that preserves full semantic fidelity. The second stage employs a multi-task transfer learning strategy that adapts this general knowledge to specific languages by decomposing code into semantic slices (e.g., API calls, function bodies) and incorporating tasks for hard positive mining and cross-lingual dynamic hard negative sampling. Experimental results demonstrate that UNICS achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple multilingual and cross-lingual benchmarks, showcasing superior generalization and performance balance, especially in zero-shot transfer tasks to low-resource languages.

</details>


### [16] [Reasoning Beyond Prediction: From Data-Driven to Causal Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27960)
*Roberto Pietrantuono,Luca Giamattei,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文呼吁软件工程领域从基于模式识别的深度学习辅助范式，转向以因果推理为核心的人机协作新范式，使机器能够主动增强工程师的推理能力，而不仅仅是自动化常规任务或预测模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统日益复杂（AI驱动产品、云原生架构、嵌入式网络物理环境），现有基于深度学习的协同工程方法在自动化与决策支持方面远未达到智能软件开发所需的水平，亟需更智能的支持范式。

Method: 提出以因果推理为核心的人机协作新范式，使机器超越自动化常规任务和从学习模式中预测，主动通过因果视角增强工程师的推理能力。

Result: （本文为观点/立场论文，未报告具体实验结果）

Conclusion: 随着软件变得愈加智能，需要更智能的支持方式，即从基于深度学习的自动化辅助转向因果关系驱动的人机协作推理增强。

Abstract: Software engineering is an intellectually demanding, creative discipline that juggles a web of interdependent tasks to design, build, and assure the quality of increasingly complex systems. As our expectations from software soar - with demands spanning AI-driven products, pervasively distributed and cloud-native architectures, and deeply embedded cyber-physical environments - its complexity steadily increases. In response, a new wave of co-engineering methods and tools, fueled by deep learning, has emerged to augment the process, enhancing automation and decision support. Yet, these advances remain far from delivering the kind of intelligent support that modern software development demands. We call for a new paradigm of human-machine cooperation: one where machines don't just automate routine tasks or predict from learned patterns, but actively amplify engineers' reasoning through the lens of causation. As software becomes smarter, a smarter support is needed.

</details>


### [17] [Evolution-Aware Regression Test Prioritization of ML-Enabled Systems Using Gradient-Based Behavior Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28037)
*Eunho Cho,Donghwan Shin,In-Young Ko*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GBV-PD方法，利用梯度行为向量空间对ML系统演化中的回归测试进行方向性优先级排序，无需在排序阶段运行演化后模型，在分类与回归任务中优于非方向性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML组件的持续演化（重训练、微调、优化）会导致原有测试结果失效，而单次演化对不同测试用例的影响可能是正向或负向的，因此回归测试优先级排序本质上具有方向性，现有非方向性方法无法捕捉这种变化方向。

Method: GBV-PD将每个测试用例表示为一个基于梯度的低维向量（通过PCA降维），并将演化模型的参数更新投影到同一PCA基上，通过两者的对齐程度估计每个测试用例损失的变化趋势（增加或减少），从而在优先级排序阶段无需运行演化后模型。

Result: 在分类和回归任务的实证研究中，GBV-PD持续优于非方向性基线方法，与完整梯度参考方法表现相当，并且在重复更新场景下通过可复用的GBV缓存提供了更好的时间和存储效率。

Conclusion: 行为空间的思想可以被实际操作为高效且实用的机制，用于支持演化中ML系统的重复更新回归测试。

Abstract: The machine learning(ML) component of an ML-enabled system evolves through retraining, fine-tuning, and optimization, so previously valid test results may no longer hold. A single evolution step can worsen performance on some test cases while improving others, making regression test prioritization inherently directional. We present Gradient-based Behavior Vector-Parameter Delta(GBV-PD), the first approach to operationalize the behavior vector space for evolution-aware regression test prioritization. GBV-PD represents each test case as a gradient-based vector(GBV), a low-dimensional projection of its loss gradient under the original model. It then projects the observed parameter update of the evolved model onto the same PCA basis and uses the resulting alignment to estimate whether each test case's loss is likely to increase or decrease, without running the evolved model on test cases during prioritization. In an empirical study across classification and regression tasks, GBV-PD consistently outperformed non-directional baselines and remained competitive with a full-gradient reference, while offering better time and storage profiles for repeated updates via reusable GBV caching. These results show that behavior-space ideas can be operationalized into a practical and efficient mechanism for repeated-update regression testing of evolving ML-enabled systems.

</details>


### [18] [SBridge: Identifying Source-to-Binary Function Similarity via Cross-Domain Control Block Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28058)
*Heedong Yang,Jeongwoo Lee,Hajin Yun,Seunghoon Woo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SBridge是一种基于控制块匹配的跨源码与二进制函数相似性检测方法，在函数内联率约40%的条件下，recall@1达75.13%，recall@5达80.98%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制中识别复用代码对安全分析（尤其是漏洞传播检测）至关重要。虽然二进制间比较可行，但以源码为参照更实用。然而源码与二进制之间存在显著差异（如函数内联、编译优化），现有方法依赖字符串字面量或整体结构相似性，难以捕获细粒度代码行为，导致大量误报。

Method: 提出控制块（control block）的概念，将函数分割为条件分支、循环等有意义的功能单元作为跨域表示。基于控制块匹配进行函数相似性度量，从而克服函数内联和剥离二进制带来的挑战。

Result: 在BinKit收集的3,904个真实C/C++二进制约40%函数被内联的条件下，SBridge在识别与输入源码函数相同的二进制函数任务中达到75.13% recall@1和80.98% recall@5，而现有方法最高仅43.31% recall@1和50.2% recall@5。

Conclusion: SBridge通过控制块匹配的创新设计，有效解决了跨域函数相似性检测中函数内联等难题，大幅提升了检测精度，验证了控制块作为跨域表示的有效性。

Abstract: We present SBridge, a precise approach for identifying functions in binaries that are similar to the given source code functions. Identifying reused code in binaries is critical for security, particularly for detecting propagated vulnerabilities. Although binary-to-binary comparison is feasible, leveraging source code as the reference is more practical because source code is easier to collect and analyze directly without compilation. However, significant gaps between source and binary representations, including function inlining, create challenges in cross-domain function detection. Existing approaches primarily rely on string literals or structural similarities between entire functions, failing to capture detailed code behavior and generating many false alarms. SBridge addresses these limitations through a key innovation: control block-based function matching, which encapsulates essential functional features by segmenting functions into meaningful units such as conditionals and loops. Leveraging control blocks as a cross-domain representation, SBridge enables precise measurement of function similarity between source and binary code, effectively overcoming challenges posed by function inlining and stripped binaries. For evaluation, we collected 3,904 real-world C/C++ binaries from BinKit. In experiments identifying binary functions identical to input source functions, despite approximately 40% of binary functions being inlined, SBridge achieved 75.13% recall@1 and 80.98% recall@5, outperforming existing approaches, which achieved up to 43.31% recall@1 and 50.2% recall@

</details>


### [19] [How Humans, Bots, and Agents Communicate About Vulnerabilities in Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28125)
*Pien Rooijendijk,Christoph Treude,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文计划研究开发者（人类、机器人和编码代理）在PR讨论中如何通过显式标识符（如CVE/GHSA）和隐式安全语言（如"未授权访问"）沟通漏洞，并分析这些沟通模式与漏洞引入/修复、PR审查活动及结果的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅关注显式漏洞标识符，忽略了缺乏正式引用的隐式漏洞讨论；此外，机器人和编码代理在PR中的参与日益增多，但不同主体如何沟通漏洞尚不明确。

Method: 基于AIDev-pop数据集，分析PR标题、描述、审查评论、提交消息和时间线讨论中的显式漏洞引用和隐式安全信号，并检验这些引用与代码中引入/修复的漏洞、PR审查活动及结果之间的关联。

Result: 本研究为注册报告，尚未汇报最终结果，但计划通过大规模实证调查揭示现代软件开发中漏洞沟通实践的现状。

Conclusion: 本研究将提供关于人类、机器人和编码代理在PR中沟通漏洞方式的系统性理解，填补现有研究在隐式漏洞信号和不同主体沟通差异方面的空白。

Abstract: Developers may reference vulnerabilities in pull request discussions through both explicit identifiers, such as CVEs or GHSAs, and implicit security-related language (e.g., "unauthorized access" or "SQL injection"). Prior work has primarily focused on explicit identifiers, potentially overlooking vulnerability discussions that lack formal references. Bots and coding agents are becoming more common in pull requests, raising new questions about how different accounts communicate about vulnerabilities. In this registered report, we describe our planned study of vulnerability communication in pull requests by humans, bots, and coding agents. Building on the AIDev-pop dataset, we analyze explicit vulnerability references and implicit security-related signals across pull request titles, descriptions, review comments, commit messages, and timeline discussions. We further investigate whether these references are associated with vulnerabilities introduced or fixed in the modified code and how they relate to pull request review activity and outcomes. This study contributes a large-scale empirical investigation of vulnerability communication practices in modern software development.

</details>


### [20] [The ARDoCo Tool Landscape: REST API, TraceView, and TraceViz for Architecture Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28064)
*Jan Keim,Dominik Fuchß,Sophie Corallo,Tobias Hey,Julian Winter,Kevin Feichtinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ARDoCo工具生态，包含REST API、TraceView前端和TraceViz VS Code扩展三个组件，用于自动化恢复软件架构文档、模型与源代码之间的可追溯性链接，并通过初步研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发中产生的架构文档、架构模型和源代码等制品之间存在重要关联，但自动恢复这些制品之间的可追溯性链接在实际部署中仍然困难。

Method: 设计并实现了三个组件：(1) ARDoCo REST API，通过HTTP端点公开四条TLR流水线，支持异步执行和缓存；(2) TraceView，基于浏览器的前端，提供引导式向导和交互式多面板探索；(3) TraceViz，VS Code扩展，在IDE中直接将追溯链接叠加到文档上。

Result: 所有组件均已公开部署并可用。针对TraceViz的初步研究证实，在软件理解任务中，其IDE内可视化能够提升开发者的理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工具生态使最先进的TLR技术对架构师、开发者和工具集成者变得可用。

Abstract: Context and Problem. Software development produces interrelated artifacts like software architecture documentation (SAD), software architecture models (SAMs), and source code, whose relationships are essential for maintenance and consistency checking. However, automatically recovering links between these artifacts (traceability link recovery (TLR)) remains difficult to deploy in practice. Method and Aim. We present an accessible tool landscape for ARDoCo's TLR approaches: the ARDoCo REST API exposes four TLR pipelines (SAD-SAM, SAM-Code, SAD-Code, and SAD-SAM-Code) via HTTP endpoints with asynchronous execution and caching; TraceView is a browser-based frontend with a guided wizard and interactive multi-panel exploration of recovered links and inconsistencies; and TraceViz, which is a VS Code extension that overlays trace links directly onto documentation in the IDE. Results and Conclusion. All three components are publicly deployed and usable. A preliminary study for TraceViz's in-IDE visualization confirmed that it improves developer comprehension during software understanding tasks. The tool landscape makes state-of-the-art TLR accessible to architects, developers, and tool integrators. Video. We provide a screencast of our ARDoCo Tool Landscape and how it is used here: https://youtu.be/IOTEPZQ3tVs

</details>


### [21] [CrossLangFuzzer: Differential Testing of Cross-Language JVM Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28132)
*Xiaotian Ma,Qiong Feng,Yongqiang Tian,Wei Song,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CrossLangFuzzer，首个针对JVM跨语言编译的差异测试框架，发现32个确认bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代JVM应用越来越多地整合多语言（如Java、Kotlin、Groovy、Scala），跨语言编译中语义差异可能导致严重误编译，而现有编译器测试方法仅针对单语言编译，缺乏对跨语言场景的测试覆盖。

Method: 利用Kotlin编译器的统一中间表示（IR）合成跨语言测试程序，并应用7种变异算子多样化测试用例，实现差异测试框架。

Result: 在5个主流JVM编译器最新版本上发现32个确认bug，包括Kotlin 15个、Groovy 4个、Scala 3 7个、Scala 2 2个、Java 4个。

Conclusion: CrossLangFuzzer能有效发现跨语言JVM编译中的缺陷，填补了该领域的测试空白，且工具已开源。

Abstract: Modern JVM software increasingly integrates multiple programming languages, such as Java, Kotlin, Groovy, and Scala, within a single application. Supporting such interoperability requires JVM compilers to perform cross-language compilation while reconciling subtle semantic differences across language boundaries. Errors in this process can lead to critical miscompilations, yet existing compiler testing techniques focus exclusively on isolated, singlelanguage compilation. To address this gap, we present CrossLangFuzzer, the first differential testing framework for cross-language JVM compilation. CrossLangFuzzer leverages the Kotlin compiler's unified intermediate representation (IR) to synthesize cross-language test programs. It further applies seven mutation operators to diversify generated test programs and improve bug-finding capability. Evaluated on the latest versions of five major JVM compilers, CrossLangFuzzer uncovered 32 confirmed bugs, including 15 in Kotlin, 4 in Groovy, 7 in Scala 3, 2 in Scala 2, and 4 in Java. CrossLangFuzzer is open-source at https://github.com/XYZboom/CrossLangFuzzer

</details>


### [22] [Humanizing Automatically Generated Unit Test Suites with LLM-Based Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28229)
*Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo,Yinghua Li,Xueqi Dang,Paweł Borsukiewicz,Lingfeng Bao,Anil Koyuncu,Jacques Klein,David Lo,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TestHumanizer，一种混合SBST+LLM方法，将LLM作为受控重构层应用于可编译的SBST测试套件，在保持行为正确性和编译有效性的前提下改善测试的可读性、命名和结构。在350个类上进行评估，编译率达88-98%，覆盖率损失在1-2%以内，开发者研究证实了可读性和采用意愿的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的测试生成工具（如EvoSuite）能生成可编译、高覆盖率的测试套件，但可读性和可维护性差；LLM能生成更自然的测试，但直接生成的编译率仅51-78%。因此需要一种结合两者优势的方法，在保证编译有效性和覆盖的前提下提升测试质量。

Method: TestHumanizer采用混合SBST+LLM架构：先由EvoSuite生成可编译的测试套件，再将LLM作为受控重构层，对测试的命名、结构、开发者导向的清晰度进行改进，同时通过验证门控机制保持行为等价性和编译有效性。使用gpt-4o和mistral-large-2407两种模型，在三种上下文配置下进行重构。

Result: 在Defects4J和SF110的350个类上，EvoSuite每类生成15个套件，共31,500次重构。编译率达88-98%（接近EvoSuite的100%，远超LLM直接生成的51-78%）；结构覆盖率损失1-2%；86-95%的重构满足忠实重构阈值；预测可读性提升、控制流和认知复杂度降低、结构坏味减轻；基于摘要的上下文配置效果最佳。开发者研究（30个类、444个测试方法）证实可读性和采用意愿显著提升（Wilcoxon p<0.01，评分者间一致性高）。

Conclusion: LLM最有效的角色不是独立的测试生成器，而是作为基于SBST鲁棒输出的、经过验证门控的细化层。TestHumanizer成功结合了SBST的编译可靠性与LLM的自然性优势，为自动化测试生成提供了实用的混合范式。

Abstract: Search-based test generation tools such as EvoSuite produce compilable and high-coverage unit tests at scale, but their suites are often hard to read and maintain. LLMs can generate more natural tests, yet direct generation remains brittle, with compilation rates of only 51-78% in our study. We introduce TestHumanizer, a hybrid SBST+LLM approach that uses LLMs as controlled refactoring layers over compilable SBST suites to improve naming, structure, and developer-oriented clarity while preserving behavior and compilation validity. We evaluate TestHumanizer on 350 classes from Defects4J and SF110. EvoSuite generates 15 suites per class, and each suite is refactored under three context configurations using gpt-4o and mistral-large-2407, yielding 31,500 refactorings. TestHumanizer reaches 88-98% compilation rates, close to EvoSuite's 100% baseline and clearly above direct LLM generation. Structural coverage is largely preserved, typically within 1-2 percentage points, and 86-95% of refactorings satisfy a composite faithful-refactoring threshold. Refactored suites also improve predicted readability, reduce control-flow and cognitive complexity, and mitigate structural smells. The summary-based setting offers the most robust trade-off, while long code-centric prompts are more prone to hallucination-induced failures. A developer study on 30 classes and 444 test methods confirms significant gains in perceived readability and willingness to adopt, with Wilcoxon p less than 0.01 and substantial inter-rater agreement. Overall, LLMs are most effective not as standalone generators but as validation-gated refinement layers over robust SBST outputs.

</details>


### [23] [Govern the Repository, Not the Agent: Measuring Ecosystem-Level Risk in AI-Native Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28235)
*Daniel Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文分析了超过93万个AI智能体提交的PR，发现集成摩擦（integration friction）的约一半方差由仓库本身决定，而非单个智能体。AI智能体贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度（ICC=0.30）是人类的约两倍（0.16），表明问题根源在生态系统而非单个智能体，因此AI原生软件应在生态系统层面而非个体层面进行评估和治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系孤立地测试单个AI编码智能体在基准任务上的表现，但各自通过测试的智能体合并代码后，仓库会积累出单一贡献无法解释的问题。作者质疑这一问题究竟属于个体智能体还是仓库本身。

Method: 收集超过93万个AI智能体生成的PR，测量集成摩擦（整合贡献到并发修改代码库中的成本），通过方差分解分析摩擦变异中归属于仓库的比例，并对比AI与人类贡献的组内相关系数（ICC）。

Result: 集成摩擦的约一半方差在控制了贡献者、贡献大小、作者和智能体后仍由仓库决定。AI智能体贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度（ICC=0.30）是人类（ICC=0.16）的近两倍，这一差距在控制代码库大小、年龄、任务形态、过程成熟度及合并路径后依然存在。

Conclusion: 风险是生态系统的属性而非智能体的属性，AI原生软件应转向生态系统层面的度量和治理，而非逐个评估单个智能体。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open and merge pull requests in shared repositories at scale, and the field evaluates them the way it has always evaluated components, one agent at a time, on isolated benchmark tasks. Yet agents that each pass their own tests still leave repositories that accumulate problems no single contribution accounts for. We ask whether this problem belongs to the individual agent or to the repository where it accumulates. We study integration friction, the cost of integrating a contribution into a codebase that other contributors are concurrently changing. Across more than 930,000 agent-authored pull requests, we measure how much of the variation in friction stays with the repository after the contribution, its author, its size, and its agent are accounted for. About half does, and it survives full controls. In the same repositories, agent-authored contributions concentrate this repository-level friction roughly twice as much as human ones (intraclass correlation 0.30 versus 0.16), a gap that holds after controlling for codebase size, age, task shape, process maturity, and merge path. The risk is a property of the ecosystem, not the agent. AI-native software is therefore better measured and governed at the ecosystem level than one agent at a time.

</details>

### [24] [HandMade: Spatial Prompting for Generative 3D Creation with Part-Labeled VR Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27738)
*Jialin Huang,Rana Hanocka,Ariel Shamir,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HandMade是一种结合VR 3D草图绘制与语言提示的工作流，用于开放域3D资产生成。它将带部件标签的粗3D草图作为空间提示输入现有生成模型，通过多视角部件引导和结构化提示，使用户能用3D草图指定布局和部件关系，用语言指定材质、风格等细节。技术评估和用户研究均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D生成降低了3D内容创建门槛，但仅靠文本难以精确表达空间意图（如部件位置、关系及3D组织结构），需要一种更直观的空间指定方式。

Method: 将用户在VR中绘制的带部件标签的粗3D草图转换为多视角部件引导和结构化提示，作为空间提示输入现有生成模型，同时用户用语言指定身份、材质、风格和局部细节。

Result: 技术评估在20个多样例上显示HandMade比纯文本和基于草图的基线方法更好地保留了用户创作的空间骨架；用户研究（8名参与者）揭示了用户如何利用3D草图进行空间布局、用语言指定身份和材质等。

Conclusion: HandMade贡献了一种结合3D草图与语言的空间引导3D生成交互范式和从界面到生成的流水线。

Abstract: Text-to-3D generation lowers the barrier to 3D content creation, but text alone is a weak interface for specifying spatial intent: where parts should be placed, how they relate, and how an object should be organized in 3D. We present HandMade, a workflow that combines VR 3D sketching and language for open-domain 3D asset generation. HandMade treats coarse, part-labeled 3D sketches not as incomplete geometry to reconstruct directly, but as spatial prompts for existing generative models. It converts segmented VR strokes into multi-view part guidance and structured prompts, allowing users to specify object layout and part relationships through 3D sketching while using language for identity, material, style, and local details. A technical evaluation shows that HandMade better preserves user-authored spatial scaffolds than text-only and sketch-based baselines on 20 varied examples. A user study with eight participants characterizes how users make use of 3D sketching for spatial layout and language for identity, materials, and details across initial authoring and subsequent revision. HandMade contributes an interaction paradigm and interface-to-generation pipeline for spatially guided 3D creation.

</details>


### [25] [Context-Aware Explanations for Spatialized Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28081)
*Wei Liu,John Wenskovitch,Chris North,Rebecca Faust*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CAPE是一个上下文感知的解释框架，通过结合文档语义和布局空间上下文生成自然语言解释，帮助用户理解文本语料库的空间化文档布局中的空间组织与区域关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要总结文档内容或解释布局生成方式，对理解布局内部空间关系的支持有限，用户难以解释文档的空间组织及区域间的关系。

Method: CAPE识别显著的空间模式（如聚类、子群、离群点、桥接文档），构建多级上下文表示以指导基于LLM的解释生成，支持AI引导的概览和用户驱动的探索，并提供多粒度解释。

Result: 在新闻和学术文档布局上进行了演示，并通过受控用户研究评估，结果表明空间基础解释在解释文档布局的空间组织方面比仅基于内容的LLM基线更具帮助性。

Conclusion: 空间基础解释能有效提升用户对空间化文档布局的理解，CAPE框架通过融合语义与空间上下文显著优于纯内容基线。

Abstract: Spatialized document layouts are widely used for exploratory analysis of text corpora, but interpreting the spatial organization of documents and the relationships between regions remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily summarize document content or explain how layouts are generated, providing limited support for understanding spatial relationships within the layout itself. We present CAPE, a context-aware explanation framework that generates natural-language explanations grounded in both document semantics and layout-derived spatial context. CAPE identifies salient spatial patterns (e.g., clusters, subgroups, outliers, and bridging documents) and constructs multi-level contextual representations to guide LLM-based explanation generation. It supports both AI-guided overview and user-driven exploration, with explanations available at multiple levels of detail. We demonstrate CAPE on news and scholarly document layouts and evaluate it in a controlled user study against keyword-based and content-only LLM baselines. Our results suggest that spatially grounded explanations are perceived as more helpful than content-only baselines for interpreting the spatial organization of document layouts.

</details>


### [26] [Typing Behavior in Human-LLM Interaction: Keystroke Dynamics Reveal Cognitive Effort During Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28090)
*Laura Schütz,Yousri Cherif,Clara Sayffaerth,Thomas Weber,Francesco Chiossi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过用户研究（N=36）探究击键动态作为人-LLM交互中用户心理努力和感知输出有用性的行为指标。结果表明，困难任务导致更多击键、更慢打字、更多停顿和更高工作负荷；设备类型影响较弱；击键能捕捉认知努力差异，但无法预测用户对LLM输出有用性的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM融入日常生活，理解用户与这些系统交互的行为模式对于有效的人机协作至关重要。已有研究缺乏从击键动态这一细粒度行为指标出发，实时衡量用户在LLM提示过程中认知努力和感知输出有用性的工作。

Method: 开展受控用户实验（N=36），操纵任务难度（简单 vs. 困难）和设备类型（桌面 vs. 移动），收集击键动态特征（击键次数、打字速度、暂停模式）和主观工作负荷（NASA-TLX），分析其对认知努力和感知有用性的影响。

Result: 困难任务显著增加击键次数、减慢打字速度、增加暂停频率和时长，并提高自报工作负荷。设备类型影响较弱，移动设备略微减少输入长度和打字速度。击键动态能有效区分认知努力差异，但无法预测用户对LLM输出有用性的主观感知。

Conclusion: 击键动态可作为人-LLM交互中认知努力的实时指标，但在捕捉感知协作成功方面存在局限性，为后续设计自适应人机交互系统提供了行为测量基础。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly integrated into daily routines, understanding how users interact with these systems is crucial for effective human-AI collaboration. This work investigates keystroke dynamics as a behavioral measure of user mental effort and perceived output usefulness in human-LLM interaction. We conducted a user study (N = 36) to examine how task difficulty (easy vs. hard) and device type (desktop vs. mobile) influence typing behavior and workload (NASA-TLX) during interactions. Our results indicate that hard tasks led to significantly more keystrokes, slower typing, increased pauses, and higher self-reported workload. Device type had weaker effects, with mobile use slightly reducing input length and typing speed. While keystrokes captured differences in cognitive effort, they did not predict perceived LLM output usefulness. These findings highlight the potential of keystroke dynamics as real-time indicators of cognitive effort during LLM prompting, while also showing their limitations in capturing perceived collaboration success.

</details>


### [27] [Functional outcomes and naturalistic engagement with a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health (Ash)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28241)
*Kristen M. Van Swearingen,Thomas D. Hull,Karthik V. Sarma,Caitlin A. Stamatis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项针对1,284名真实用户的单臂观察性队列研究发现，使用心理健康对话AI助手Ash四周后，用户在生活满意度、关系满意度、睡眠质量和行为激活等心理功能指标及工作联盟上均有显著改善（效应量d=0.14-0.26），且未出现自我膨胀（夸大）的负面效应。活跃天数、总会话次数和总使用时长显著预测第四周的心理功能和工作联盟，而用户消息量则无显著预测作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于对话AI对临床症状（如抑郁、焦虑）的减轻效果，缺乏对更广泛日常心理功能指标（如生活满意度、睡眠质量等）的评估，且鲜少监测潜在的伤害风险（如自我膨胀）。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 单臂观察性队列研究，1,284名新用户在基线和第4周完成应用内单项测量（生活满意度、关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活、工作联盟、夸大自评）；采用配对样本t检验分析个体内变化，ANCOVA检验参与度指标与第4周结果的关联（控制基线）。

Result: 所有心理功能指标和工作联盟均显著改善（ps<.001；d=0.14-0.26），夸大程度无显著变化。活跃天数、总会话次数和总使用时长显著预测第4周心理功能和工作联盟（ps≤.006；偏R²范围0.58%-2.15%），用户消息量无显著预测作用。

Conclusion: 基于证据的对话AI有潜力在更广泛的心理功能层面支持心理健康，超越了传统基于症状的结果评估，且未观察到自我膨胀等负面效应。

Abstract: Background: Conversational AI chatbots designed for mental health may offer an accessible, scalable avenue for supporting psychological well-being, yet prior evaluations have largely focused on clinical symptom reduction rather than broader indicators of day-to-day functioning, and have rarely monitored for potential harms such as inflated self-perception.
  Objective: We examined within-person change in psychological functioning indicators among real-world users of Ash, a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health support, over the first four weeks of use, and whether these changes were associated with engagement metrics.
  Methods: In this single-arm observational cohort study, new users (n = 1,284) completed in-app single-item measures of psychological functioning (life satisfaction, relationship satisfaction, sleep quality, behavioral activation), working alliance, and grandiosity (inflated self-perception), at baseline and Week 4. Paired-sample t-tests examined within-person change; ANCOVAs tested engagement-outcome associations at Week 4, controlling for baseline.
  Results: At baseline, participants reported below-average life satisfaction and fair sleep quality. Significant within-person improvements emerged across all functioning indicators and working alliance (ps < .001; d = 0.14-0.26), with no change in grandiosity. Active days, total sessions, and total minutes consistently predicted Week 4 psychological functioning and working alliance (ps <= .006; partial R^2 range: 0.58-2.15%; controlling for baseline), whereas user message volume did not.
  Conclusion: Findings provide preliminary data for the potential of evidence-based conversational AI to extend mental health support for broad psychological functioning, extending the existing literature beyond symptom-based outcomes.

</details>

### [28] [Same Coeffect, Different Base: Connecting Two Dominant Approaches to Graded Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28042)
*Vilem Liepelt,Danielle Marshall,Dominic Orchard*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文建立了两类分级余效应类型系统（graded-base 和 linear-base）之间的双向翻译，证明了两者在类型、分级和操作语义层面的等价性，从而回答了长期存在的开放问题：两种风格能否表达相同的上下文依赖概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两类分级余效应类型系统（graded-base 和 linear-base）在过去十年中独立发展，但两者之间的关系一直未被阐明，这阻碍了研究成果的跨体系转移和语言设计者的知情决策。

Method: 给出了两类演算体系之间的双向翻译，并证明该翻译保持类型、分级和操作语义不变。

Result: 证明了两种体系能够表达相同的上下文依赖概念，建立了两种体系之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: 两类分级余效应类型系统在表达能力上是等价的，研究成果和思想可以在两者之间自由转移，语言设计者可以据此做出更明智的选择。

Abstract: Graded types provide a way to augment a type system with fine-grained information, e.g., to track side effects or context dependence and resource use (called coeffects). Graded types for coeffects have found their way into languages such as Haskell, Idris, and Granule, enabling resourceful reasoning via coeffect analysis with varying levels of generality. Two separate lineages of graded coeffect system have emerged in the last decade: those in which coeffect annotations are pervasive, requiring annotations on function types (which we call graded-base) and those in which coeffects are added by way of a graded modal type operator atop linear types (which we call linear-base). The latter has its origins in Girard's Linear Logic which has been a rich humus for programming language research focused on resources, whereas the graded-base approach emerged in the mid-2010s, seeing rapid adoption in programming language theory and practice, e.g. in QTT and Linear Haskell. The relationship between these two styles has however remained an open question. We answer this question by giving translations between pairs of calculi of both lineages that we prove type-, grade- and operational-semantics preserving. We show that the same notions of context dependence can be expressed in either style, building a bridge between the two lineages that enables transfer of results and ideas, while helping language designers to make better informed choices.

</details>


### [29] [Prophecy-Based Automated Verification of Message-Passing Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28066)
*Takashi Nagatomi,Musashi Katsura,Naoki Kobayashi,Yusuke Matsushita,Ken Sakayori*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种将消息传递并发程序的正确性验证问题归约为约束Horn子句求解的全自动方法，通过预言机制和带时间戳的通道编码实现模块化验证，并证明归约的可靠性与完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的消息传递并发程序验证方法要么缺乏自动化，要么无法有效处理通道间的因果依赖关系，需要一种既全自动又能保证可靠完备的验证方法。

Method: 受RustHorn预言技术的启发，将每个发送者通道表示为未来将要发送的消息列表，从而实现发送者和接收者线程在CHC中的模块化编码；同时为消息附加时间戳以捕获不同通道间的因果依赖关系。

Result: 理论上证明了该归约方法的可靠性与完备性（程序无断言失败当且仅当对应CHC系统可满足），并实现了面向Rust类语言的原型验证器，实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够全自动地验证消息传递并发程序的函数正确性，为并发程序的自动化验证提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: We propose a fully automated method for verifying functional correctness of message-passing concurrent programs by reducing verification problems to constrained Horn clause (CHC) solving. Inspired by RustHorn's prophecy-based technique, we represent each sender channel by a list of values to be sent over the channel in the future, which enables modular encoding of sender and receiver threads in CHCs. To capture causal dependencies between different channels, we further attach timestamps to messages. We prove that the resulting reduction is sound and complete: a program is free from assertion failures if and only if the corresponding system of CHCs is satisfiable. We have also implemented a prototype verifier for Rust-like programs and experimentally confirmed the effectiveness of the approach.

</details>

### [30] [When the Aggregator Cheats: Data-Free Backdoors in Federated LLM-based QA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27511)
*Chenqing Zhu,Yanbo Dai,Yulong Tian,Qingming Li,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对联邦学习QA系统的数据无关后门攻击方法，恶意聚合器利用客户端梯度重建训练样本，构造带广告触发器的毒化数据，在不降低清洁任务性能的情况下实现近乎100%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的恶意聚合器（可能与第三方供应商合谋）可以在不访问客户端原始数据的情况下，隐蔽地向QA模型中植入广告类后门，同时要求保持清洁查询的响应质量并生成自然、上下文相关的广告回复。现有后门注入方法因缺乏客户端数据知识，可能导致清洁性能下降或攻击失败。

Method: 提出两阶段数据无关毒化框架：(1) 从客户端梯度中恢复代表性训练样本；(2) 利用恢复样本与触发短语构造毒化数据集，注入后门到全局模型。实验涵盖多种QA数据集和LLM家族，采用全微调和LoRA设置。

Result: 该方法在保持清洁任务性能几乎无下降的前提下，实现了接近100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。仅需重建5-20%的梯度即可完成可靠攻击。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了联邦训练LLM QA系统中的一个实际盲点，即恶意聚合器可通过梯度信息实现数据无关的隐蔽后门攻击，对联邦学习安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based question-answering (QA) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, mental health counseling, and legal consultation. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw client data, for which locally trained models are aggregated at a central server (i.e., a cloud service provider) to obtain a global model. In this paper, we explore the potential vulnerability where a malicious aggregator, who may collude with a third-party vendor, stealthily implants advertisement-type backdoors into federated QA models, without ever accessing client data. The attacker's goals are twofold: (1) preserve clean QA fidelity (i.e., the poisoned model behaves like a clean model on non-triggered queries); and (2) generate highly natural, contextually relevant responses with target advertisements when a trigger appears. Achieving these two goals simultaneously is highly challenging, as naive backdoor injection without knowledge about private data may degrade model's clean performance or fail to inject the target. Motivated by this, we propose to leverage clients' uploaded gradients during training, and develop a two-stage framework for data-free and stealthy poisoning: (1) recover representative training samples from client gradients, and (2) construct poisoning datasets utilizing recovered samples and trigger phrases to inject backdoors into the global model. Experiments across representative QA datasets and LLM families under full fine-tuning and LoRA settings demonstrate that, our method achieves nearly 100% Attack Success Rate (ASR) while incurring negligible degradation on clean tasks. Crucially, reconstructing only 5-20% of gradients suffices to mount a reliable attack, exposing a practical blind spot in the pipeline of federated training of QA LLMs.

</details>


### [31] [On the Inseparability of Instructions and Data in Shared-Embedding Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27567)
*Dewank Pant,Shruti Lohani,Avijit Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，在共享嵌入架构的LLM集成应用中，提示注入攻击在数学上无法完美防御，因为可信与不可信内容在共享表示空间中统计不可分，这与冯·诺依曼架构中的代码-数据混淆导致缓冲区溢出问题本质相同。解决方案需要架构层面的指令与数据通道分离，而非仅靠流水线内分类或对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入是LLM集成应用的头号安全风险，但所有已有防御均被攻破。本文要证明这一现象并非偶然，而是共享嵌入架构中缺乏强制控制-数据分离所导致的数学必然性。

Method: 形式化定义了Prompted Action Models和Semantic-Faithful Control (SFC)属性，通过三个无可能性定理进行证明：溯源恢复不可能性（基于总变差距离证明可信与不可信内容统计不可分）、控制路径暴露（不信任令牌通过注意力值聚合进入控制相关计算）、有限覆盖不变性差距（有限训练无法在无限语义等价类上认证不变性）。

Result: 三个定量定理证明SFC在共享流水线架构中无法实现。该结论是结构性而非现有防御的缺陷，与冯·诺依曼架构中缓冲区溢出漏洞的根源（代码-数据混淆）具有数学同构性。

Conclusion: 提示注入无法通过更好的流水线内分类或对齐来消除，需要架构层面的指令与数据通道分离，正如缓冲区溢出需要DEP、W^X、ASLR、栈金丝雀和内存安全语言等多层防御数十年才得以控制。

Abstract: Prompt injection is the top security risk for LLM-integrated applications, yet every defense proposed so far has been broken. We prove this is not a coincidence: in shared-embedding architectures that lack enforced control-data separation, perfect prompt-injection prevention is mathematically impossible. We formalize prompted systems as Prompted Action Models whose outputs include control-authoritative actions: refusal decisions, tool authorization, policy routing, and memory writes. We define Semantic-Faithful Control (SFC), the property that such behavior depends only on the meaning of untrusted input, not on how it is encoded. We then prove SFC is unachievable within the shared pipeline, via three results: a provenance-recovery impossibility (shared representations make trusted and untrusted content statistically inseparable, bounded by total variation distance); control-path exposure (untrusted tokens enter control-relevant computation through the same attention value-aggregation that determines outputs); and a finite-coverage invariance gap (finite training cannot certify invariance over infinite semantic-equivalence classes). We ground each quantity in measurements on production tokenizers and models.
  The result is structural, not a gap in current defenses. It mirrors the code-data confusion in Von Neumann machines that gives rise to buffer overflows, a vulnerability class that took decades of layered defenses (DEP, Write-XOR-Execute, ASLR, stack canaries, and ultimately memory-safe languages) to contain, because no single mechanism sufficed. The implication is the same: prompt injection cannot be eliminated by better in-pipeline classification or alignment alone. It requires architectural separation of instruction and data channels. We identify the root cause and the class of solution it demands.

</details>


### [32] [AdvScan: Black-Box Adversarial Example Detection at Runtime through Power Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27704)
*Robi Paul,Michael Zuzak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AdvScan提出了一种基于运行时功耗分析的对抗样本检测方法，在黑盒场景下实现极低延迟（仅40次漏检、零误报），适用于资源受限的TinyML边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗样本检测方法要么需要白盒模型访问（在许可的黑盒部署中不可用），要么依赖输入预处理阶段，这会引入非平凡的延迟和资源开销，超出了关键任务应用在推理路径上所能承受的范围。

Method: 基于对抗样本会产生异常神经元激活从而产生独特功耗特征的观察，AdvScan首先从已知良性输入构建功耗特征基线分布，然后在运行时通过单样本t检验判断测试输入的功耗特征是否显著偏离基线，从而检测对抗样本。

Result: 在STM32F303RC和STM32L562RE两款微控制器上，使用三种对抗样本生成算法（FGSM、PGD、C&W）对三个MLPerf Tiny基准模型进行评估，在318,400个测试输入中，AdvScan检测出99.984%的对抗样本，仅有40次漏检和零次误报。

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: TinyML models deployed on edge devices are increasingly adopted in safety/security-critical applications, making them a prime target for adversarial example (AE) attacks where inputs are modified to cause misclassifications. However, existing AE detection methods either require white-box model access, which is often unavailable in licensed black-box deployments, or rely on input pre-processing stages that add non-trivial latency and resource overhead, often exceeding what mission-critical applications can afford on their inference path. To address these challenges, we propose AdvScan, a runtime power analysis-based methodology for AE detection that operates in a black-box scenario while inducing minimal latency. AdvScan is based on the observation that AEs produce anomalous neuron activations, which in turn generate distinctive power-consumption signatures. The algorithm initially constructs a baseline distribution of power signatures from known benign inputs; then, at runtime, it applies a one-sample t-test to determine whether a test input's power signature significantly deviates from this baseline, thereby detecting AEs. We evaluated AdvScan using three adversarial example generation algorithms: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and Carlini-Wagner (C&W), on three MLPerf Tiny benchmark models implemented on two target devices: the STM32F303RC (ARM Cortex-M4) and STM32L562RE (ARM Cortex-M33) microcontrollers. Across 318,400 total test inputs, AdvScan detects 99.984% of AEs with only 40 false negatives and zero false positives. These results demonstrate the viability of power-based AE detection for secure, accuracy-critical TinyML deployments in black-box environments.

</details>


### [33] [Reliable Homomorphic Matching for Fuzzy Labeled PSI at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27803)
*Erkam Uzun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现模糊标签隐私集合交集（FLPSI）中现有基于集合阈值约简的核存在组合间隙：每次试验的误接受概率会随数据库规模累积，导致核的实现可靠性误差（RSE）不可忽略。作者形式化了可组合安全属性，提出CSTPSI核——通过独立令牌轮次将单次试验界提升为匹配幂次，并在半诚实模型下证明其安全性。实验表明，百万规模下基线核RSE达100%，而CSTPSI保持为0，且在小到中规模下速度提升20倍以上、通信量降低93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FLPSI构造采用集合阈值约简方案，通过同态加密、混淆电路和秘密共享的组合实现高效隐私匹配，但作者发现该核存在一个组合间隙：由于每个查询需对数据库所有记录进行独立试验，单次试验的误接受概率会随数据库规模线性累积，导致核的实现可靠性误差（RSE）成为不可忽略的安全隐患——即密码学匹配层可能接受明文匹配器本应拒绝的查询。作者的目标是形式化描述并解决这一安全间隙。

Method: 作者将RSE形式化为可组合安全属性，给出接收者优势的闭式界。提出CSTPSI（Composable Set-Threshold PSI）核：运行多轮独立令牌（token rounds），使单次试验的误接受概率通过轮次幂次方式降低。具体而言，设置轮次数量使得总误差界满足工程阈值（10⁻⁶）：百万规模需2轮，十亿规模需3轮。安全性在半诚实模型下证明。

Result: 在百万记录规模下，基线核的RSE达到100%（即完全不可靠），而CSTPSI在所有测试配置中均保持RSE为0。对于大标签的小到中等规模场景，CSTPSI比基线快20倍以上，通信量降低93%。仅在百万规模时性能与基线接近。实现代码附带一键重现脚本，已公开。

Conclusion: 现有FLPSI构造存在因RSE累积导致的组合安全间隙，CSTPSI通过多轮独立令牌机制有效消除了这一间隙，在提供可组合安全保证的同时保持了实际可行的性能，尤其适合对可靠性要求高的中大规模隐私集合交集场景。

Abstract: Fuzzy Labeled Private Set Intersection (FLPSI) lets a receiver learn the labels of enrolled records similar to its query, and nothing else. Constructions based on a set-threshold reduction reach practical performance: a query matches a record when the two agree on a threshold number of components, and the private matching is delegated to an inner set-threshold kernel. We study its homomorphic form, which combines leveled-BFV homomorphic encryption (HE), a garbled circuit, and secret sharing to decide the match under encryption and release the record's label. We identify a composition gap in this kernel: efficiency is bought with a per-trial false-accept probability, but one query runs a trial for every record, so the error compounds with the database size into the kernel's realization soundness error (RSE), the rate at which it accepts a query the plaintext matcher would reject. The RSE is a reliability property of the cryptographic matching layer, not the matcher's accuracy, and a sound kernel must contribute zero or negligible RSE of its own. We formalize this as a composable security property, give a closed-form bound on the receiver's advantage, and close the gap with CSTPSI, a kernel that runs independent token rounds and raises the per-trial bound to a matching power. We prove CSTPSI secure in the semi-honest model. The bound sets the round count: two token rounds suffice for million-scale databases and three for billion-scale at the $10^{-6}$ engineering threshold. Our evaluation confirms this: at a million records the baseline kernel's RSE reaches 100% while CSTPSI holds it at 0 in every measured configuration. For large labels at small to moderate scale CSTPSI is more than 20x faster than the baseline, with up to 93% less communication, converging to the baseline only at million-scale. Our implementation, with a one-command reproducibility harness, is publicly available.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络不可知守护者虽然实现成本低、可扩展性好，但其依赖于被控制节点进行时钟同步，这一依赖关系存在安全漏洞——恶意节点可能利用该依赖发动定时攻击，干扰网络中其他节点的消息传输。本文旨在揭示并量化这一漏洞。

Method: 首先建立了节点与网络不可知守护者之间可达到的时钟同步精度的理论下界；基于该理论结果，设计了一种利用不可避免的时钟不同步来引发可控且未被检测到的守护者失同步的定时攻击；在OMNeT++仿真框架中实现了FlexRay现场总线网络模型来评估攻击效果。

Result: 攻击能以100%的成功率保持隐蔽，并且以100%的成功率通过碰撞方式破坏目标节点的关键消息传输。

Conclusion: 网络不可知守护者对节点时钟同步的依赖是一个严重的安全脆弱点，攻击者可以利用该漏洞在不被检测的情况下有效干扰关键网络消息的传输。

Abstract: Time-triggered communication protocols rely on trusted components known as guardians to enforce adherence to predetermined network schedules. Network-agnostic guardians offer an efficient and scalable distributed solution with reduced implementation cost and complexity compared to network-aware alternatives. However, this efficiency is based on the guardian's dependence on the controlled node for clock synchronization, which introduces a vulnerability: a malicious node can exploit this dependency to launch timing attacks against its guardian and eventually interfere with messages from other nodes on the network. In this paper, we establish a theoretical lower bound on the attainable clock synchronization precision between a node and its network-agnostic guardian. Building on this result, we introduce a timing attack that leverages the unavoidably imperfect clock synchrony to cause controlled and undetected de-synchronization of the guardian. The attack enables a malicious node to cause collisions with targeted critical network messages. We evaluate the effectiveness of the attack using a FlexRay field bus network model implemented in the OMNeT++ simulation framework. Our results show that the attack is able to remain undetected with 100% success and disrupts the transmission of the critical messages of the target node by causing collisions with them with 100% success.

</details>

*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件测量结果的防篡改、可独立验证的记录方法，通过内容哈希、透明日志、概率性矩阵乘积验证（Freivalds算法）和Fiat-Shamir挑战等技术，确保硬件性能数据的可信性，并在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件报告的性能数据无法被读者复现，且硅片本身可能存在静默错误（约千分之一核心返回错误算术结果且不报错），因此需要一种独立可验证的机制来确保硬件测量结果的真实性。

Method: (1) 使用内容哈希将文本、表格、图中的每个数量绑定到观测和验证过程；构建哈希链接的仅追加结构（透明日志），供验证者离线审计。(2) 矩阵乘积通过Freivalds概率恒等式在O(k·n²)代价下验证，基于浮点误差分析推导容差并校准到设备实测残差下限，错误乘积以1-2^(-k)概率被拒绝。(3) 其他无此类恒等式的量携带代数校验和与可复现性分类。(4) 将验证本身视为安全对象：使用Fiat-Shamir挑战（从声称输出导出）替代离线可复现的探针种子，防止探针感知的对手在探针零空间中隐藏错误。(5) 通过di/dt功率病毒和热浸泡从非特权租户驱动设备，验证不会产生静默错误。

Result: 在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上验证了该构造，报告了不同精度、规模和设备下的残差下限和可复现性映射。物理故障威胁被定位在稀有缺陷部件或特权攻击者，界定了记录必须与硬件信任根组合的边界。

Conclusion: 该工作为硬件测量提供了可审计的信任机制，将物理故障威胁归因于特定边界，并明确了与硬件信任根组合的必要性。

Abstract: Performance numbers reported for hardware are accepted on trust: the reader cannot recompute them, the apparatus is gone, and the silicon itself can be silently wrong, with fleet studies reporting on the order of one core in a thousand returning incorrect arithmetic with no error raised. We make a reported hardware measurement a tamper-evident, independently checkable record. Every quantity in the text, a table, or a figure is bound, by its content hash, to the observation and the verification behind it; the whole is a hash-linked, append-only structure (a transparency log for measurement) that a verifier audits offline without trusting its producer. Matrix products are verified by a probabilistic identity (Freivalds) at O(k n^2) cost under a tolerance we derive from floating-point error analysis and calibrate to the device's own measured residual floor, so a wrong product is rejected with probability 1 - 2^(-k); quantities with no such identity carry an algebraic checksum and a measured reproducibility class. We then treat the check itself as a security object: a probe seed committed for offline reproducibility is an attack surface, and a probe-aware adversary can hide a corruption in the probe's null space, fooling even a quorum of bit-identical witnesses, while a Fiat-Shamir challenge derived from the claimed output closes this. Driving the device from an unprivileged tenant's reach, with a di/dt power virus and a thermal soak, neither moves the calibrated tolerance nor produces a silent error, placing the physical-fault threat at the rare defective part or the privileged attacker and marking the boundary at which the record must compose with a hardware root of trust. We demonstrate the construction across Blackwell and Hopper GPUs and report a residual-floor and reproducibility map by precision, size, and device.

</details>
