<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示商业数据经纪商收集的细粒度位置数据存在公众尚未广泛认识的重新识别风险，并论证智能体AI从根本上改变了这一威胁模型——传统重识别攻击需要熟练分析人员的大量人工努力，限制了实际规模，而AI代理可自动化完成这一过程。

Method: 构建了一个端到端流水线：LLM代理自主搜索开放网络，交叉引用公共记录和社交媒体，将原始坐标序列解析为候选身份，全程无需人工干预。在模拟的家庭和工作地址附近时空数据集上评估，聚焦高风险披露场景。

Result: 在25个可重识别个体中成功识别18人（72%），在全部43个案例中成功识别18个（41.9%），仅依赖时空数据和公开来源信息。

Conclusion: 实际匿名性——SDC实践的隐含基础——正在发生转变。智能体AI强化了在GDPR第26条标准下"通过任何手段合理可能实现重识别"的论点，且每个目标的成本仅为分钟级和美元级。数据保管人和监管者必须预见并应对这一近期升级的威胁。

Abstract: The widespread collection of fine-grained location data by commercial data brokers creates a re-identification risk that is not widely recognised by the public. While prior research has established that mobility traces are highly unique and that individuals can, in principle, be identified from a handful of spatio-temporal points, such attacks have historically required significant manual effort from skilled analysts, limiting their practical scale.
  In this feasibility study, we demonstrate in a real world setting that agentic AI fundamentally changes this threat model. We present an end-to-end pipeline in which large language model agents autonomously search the open web, cross-reference public records and social media, and resolve raw coordinate sequences to candidate identities - without human intervention. We evaluate the pipeline on a spatio-temporal dataset containing simulated location points anchored at and around true home and work addresses, focusing on a high-risk disclosure scenario. Our results demonstrate that, from spatio-temporal data and public sources alone, our agentic AI successfully re-identified 18 of the 25 re-identifiable individuals (72%) and 18 of 43 cases overall (41.9%).
  We discuss implications for Statistical Disclosure Control (SDC) practice and outline the near-future escalation that data custodians and regulators must anticipate. De facto anonymity - an implicit foundation of SDC practice - is shifting. Agentic AI strengthens the case that re-identification is reasonably likely by any means under the GDPR Recital-26 standard, at costs of minutes-and-dollars per target.

</details>


### [37] [Decoys Cannot Go Everywhere: Mapping the Deception Surface in MITRE ATT&CK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27966)
*Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Viliam Lisý,Harm Griffioen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对MITRE ATT&CK v18.1中全部250种攻击技术，提出四标准基础设施欺骗评估框架，发现仅80种技术（32%）支持设置攻击者可触及的诱饵，并将诱饵部署模式归纳为Sweep（扫荡）和Seek（搜寻）两类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络欺骗研究通常默认可在任何存在攻击行为的位置部署诱饵，但这一假设缺乏系统性验证。本文旨在测试该假设的适用范围，建立标准化的欺骗可行性评估方法。

Method: 提出四标准评估框架：（1）是否可部署防御者控制的诱饵；（2）攻击者是否可能与之交互；（3）交互能产生何种情报价值；（4）交互是否能可靠指示恶意行为。将该框架应用于MITRE ATT&CK v18.1全部250种技术，逐项评估并发布评估结果。

Result: 仅80种技术（32%）支持设置攻击者可能触及的诱饵；诱饵部署呈现Sweep（攻击者广域扫荡时遭遇诱饵）和Seek（攻击者搜索特定资产时遇到伪造版本）两种模式；诱饵通常具有情报价值，但交互发生率和恶意指示可靠性在不同技术间差异显著。

Conclusion: 基础设施诱饵不能假设适用于所有攻击行为，必须根据具体攻击技术评估欺骗可行性。本文提供了可审计的基线评估结果和决策规则，为未来欺骗研究和部署规划提供参考。

Abstract: Cyber deception research often assumes that a decoy can be placed wherever there is attacker behavior. This work tests that assumption across MITRE ATT&CK v18.1. We introduce a four-criterion rubric for infrastructure deception and apply it to all 250 ATT&CK techniques. The rubric evaluates whether a defender-controlled decoy can be placed, whether an attacker is likely to interact with it, what intelligence that interaction can yield, and whether the interaction reliably indicates malice. The resulting deception surface is sparse: only 80 techniques (32%) admit a decoy the attacker could plausibly reach. For the remaining 170 techniques, there is no defender-controlled asset in the attacker's path that can be fabricated as a decoy. Decoy placement across those 80 techniques falls into two patterns we call Sweep and Seek. In Sweep, the attacker moves broadly through assets in range and encounters the decoy as part of that activity. In Seek, the attacker looks for a specific kind of asset and interacts with a fabricated version of it. These patterns give a simple placement rule: a decoy must either sit on a sweep path or imitate a sought asset. We also show that decoys usually have useful intelligence potential, but whether an attacker interacts with them at all, and whether that interaction reliably indicates malice, both vary. We release the rubric, decision rules, and per-technique assessment as an auditable baseline for future deception research and deployment planning, and show that infrastructure decoys cannot be assumed to apply to all attacker behavior.

</details>


### [38] [SHARD: cell-keyed residual splitting for alignment-resistant private dense retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27976)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Shard，一种通过将嵌入向量拆分为短公开前缀和分片私有残差（在CKKS同态加密下重排序）来防御向量库泄露的攻击感知几何防御方法。该方法将攻击者所需的已知锚点数量提升约C倍（C=256时从200增至102,400），同时保持检索质量（nDCG@10），但明确承认其不是加密保证，在单细胞内部密钥会抵消、目标攻击者仅需约d_priv个锚点即可破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集嵌入向量库存在泄露风险：敌手可通过少样本对齐、零样本反转、无监督跨空间翻译等攻击从泄露的向量库中恢复原始文本。即使采用全局秘密旋转（标准轻量防御），正交Procrustes方法也能在获知约子空间维度数量的已知锚点对后恢复。需要一种能抵抗此类对齐攻击的检索保持型嵌入变换。

Method: 将中心化嵌入拆分为短公开前缀（用于第一阶段检索）和私有残差；残差被分片为C个单元，每个单元使用独立秘密密钥；在CKKS同态加密下对残差进行重排序，密钥相互抵消后内积结果精确；参数C控制从全局线性基线（C=1）到每文档微密钥（C=N）的连续设计空间。

Result: Shard返回原始空间的nDCG@10（半SVD截断会损失）；将残差映射回公共框架所需锚点数量增加约C倍（C=256时从中位数200增至102,400）；短公开前缀泄露更少的邻居结构；在微密钥极限下残差图趋于零且不可链接、可更新；抵抗学习型、非线性及无监督对齐器；在匹配效用的噪声防御几乎去匿名化每个探针时，Shard未去匿名化任何探针。

Conclusion: Shard是一种攻击感知的几何防御，而非加密保证。局限性包括：单元内密钥抵消、定向攻击者仅需约d_priv个锚点、重叠参考语料仍通过前缀泄露。通过分片密钥设计提升攻击成本，在实用性和安全性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Dense embeddings underpin semantic search and RAG, yet a leaked vector store hands much of the underlying text back to whoever holds it. The attacks that make this possible (few-shot alignment, zero-shot inversion, unsupervised cross-space translation) share one weakness: the protected store is a single global geometry that can be aligned to a known one. A secret global rotation, the usual lightweight defence, is no exception: orthogonal Procrustes recovers it once the attacker has about the subspace dimension in known pairs.
  We introduce Shard, a retrieval-preserving embedding transform that removes this weak axis. The centred embedding is split into a short public prefix (for stage-1 retrieval) and a private residual sharded into C cells under separate secret keys; the residual is reranked under CKKS, where the keys cancel and leave the inner product exact. A single parameter C runs the design from the global-linear baseline it replaces (C=1) to per-document micro-keys (C=N). Because the rerank is full-dimensional, Shard returns the raw-space nDCG@10 that half-SVD truncation gives up; and because the residual is keyed cell-locally, mapping it back to a common frame under a diffuse known-plaintext leak costs roughly C times more anchors (median 200 to 102,400 at C=256), for a few encrypted queries. The short public prefix leaks far less neighbour structure, and a micro-key limit drives the residual graph to zero with an unlinkable, renewable template. The barrier holds against learned, non-linear and unsupervised aligners, and where a matched-utility noise defence de-anonymises almost every probe, Shard de-anonymises none. We are plain about the limits: within a cell the keys cancel, a targeted attacker needs only about d_priv anchors, and an overlapping reference corpus still leaks through the prefix. Shard is an attack-aware geometric defence, not a cryptographic guarantee.

</details>

*Muris Sladić,Eman Alibalić,Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的SSH蜜罐虽能生成可信交互，但评估表明它们对坚定的攻击者仍可被识别，需要更好的框架设计来提升欺骗性。

Method: 采用多智能体、多LLM架构，包含Manager和Worker两个LLM代理；实现高级永久文件系统，允许多个并发攻击者看到相同的动态变化文件；通过单元测试、AI攻击者(ARACNE)、人类攻击者和互联网部署四种方式评估。

Result: 单元测试通过率高达99.02%；AI攻击者ARACNE难以判断是否为蜜罐（对真实Ubuntu shell也偏向于判定为蜜罐）；对人类攻击者的欺骗能力优于Cowrie，与shelLM相当；互联网部署证实了AdvancedShelLM的输出能影响真实攻击者行为。

Conclusion: AdvancedShelLM通过多代理架构和永久文件系统显著提升了蜜罐的欺骗能力，能够在真实攻击场景中有效影响攻击者行为。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots can generate believable interactions, but evaluations indicate they remain somewhat identifiable to determined attackers, indicating the need for a better scaffolding. We present a new LLM-based honeypot design that uses a multi-agent, multi-LLM architecture to address the limitations of the previous shelLM LLM honeypot. Our honeypot, called AdvancedShelLM, uses two LLM agents, a Manager and a Worker, that better understand the commands while reducing incorrect responses and increasing deception. It implements an advanced permanent filesystem, allowing many simultaneous attackers to see the same changing files for the first time. It was evaluated with: (i) unit tests for generative capabilities, (ii) an AI attacker (ARACNE) to assess realism and deception, (iii) human attackers to assess its deceptive capability, and (iv) an Internet deployment to evaluate deception in real-world attacks. In unit test results, AdvancedShelLM achieved a pass rate of up to 99.02%. The AI attacker ARACNE had issues making a decision if the system is honeypot or not, but showed slight bias towards saying honeypot, even for a real Ubuntu shell. With human attackers, AdvancedShelLM deceived more humans than Cowrie, but had similar results as shelLM. The Internet deployment showed concrete evidence that the output of AdvancedShelLM can influence the behaviour of real-life attackers.

</details>


### [40] [Ghost Without Shell: Measuring Non-Interactive SSH Attacks on Honeypots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28006)
*Veronica Valeros,Muris Sladić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过部署11个SSH蜜罐15天，发现99.23%的认证会话是非交互式的，交互式会话仅占0.10%，挑战了传统蜜罐依赖交互式shell的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证SSH蜜罐研究中关于攻击者会登录后打开交互式shell并输入命令的核心假设是否仍然成立。

Method: 部署11个SSH蜜罐，同时服务交互式和非交互式会话请求，持续15天；收集177,622个认证会话，并使用独立的Cowrie数据集进行验证。

Result: 99.23%的会话为非交互式，交互式会话仅占0.10%，且该模式在第三方数据集中得到验证。

Conclusion: 专注于交互式shell或基于会话长度与命令数量评估成功的蜜罐会遗漏绝大多数认证攻击，导致对攻击者登录后行为的错误判断。

Abstract: Cyber deception research has focused on improving honeypot deception capabilities to increase attacker engagement and extend their interactions to collect more and better intelligence. For SSH honeypots, this relies on the assumption that attackers log in, open a shell, and type. We tested whether this still held by deploying eleven SSH honeypots that served both interactive and non-interactive session requests for fifteen days. We collected 177,622 authenticated sessions and validated our results against an independent Cowrie dataset over the same time window. We found that 99.23% of sessions were non-interactive. Interactive sessions account for only 0.10%. The same pattern held in the comparative third-party dataset used for evaluation. This finding is important because a honeypot that focuses on interactive shells or evaluates success based on session length and the number of commands can miss most authenticated attacks and draw the wrong conclusions about what attackers do after login.

</details>


### [41] [ToolPrivacyBench: Benchmarking Purpose-Bound Privacy in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28061)
*Shijing Hu,Liang Liu,Zhu Meng,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToolPrivacyBench是一个评估多工具Agent工作流中隐私过度披露的基准，包含2150个案例，覆盖9种主流Agent，发现任务成功执行不意味着隐私合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数调用基准主要评估任务完成度和API正确性，隐私评估基准则关注最终响应或隐私判断，两者都无法捕捉跨多工具轨迹的目的约束信息流，因此缺乏对Agent在工具调用过程中隐私过度披露的评估能力。

Method: 构建2150个案例（1150个完全合成的隐私敏感业务流程+1000个来自现有基准的改编案例），每个案例配有策略知识库；Agent在模拟业务后端上执行后，评估器将记录的工具参数和后端审计日志与策略知识库对比，检测任务私有原子是否仅路由到授权工具和下游接收方。

Result: 对9种主流Agent的评估表明，工具执行成功并不等同于隐私披露适当：Agent可能在完成任务的同时，通过中间工具调用传递不必要的私有信息。

Conclusion: ToolPrivacyBench形式化了"按需知情"披露边界（每个工具仅接收其既定目的所需信息），并通过轨迹级审计识别多工具工作流中的隐私过度披露，为Agent隐私评估提供了新的维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly moved from standalone text generation systems to agents that invoke external tools, access environments, and execute multi-step tasks. However, conventional function-calling benchmarks mainly evaluate task completion and API correctness, while privacy evaluation benchmarks typically focus on final responses or privacy judgments. Neither perspective captures purpose-bound information flow across an executed multi-tool trajectory. Motivated by this limitation in current agent evaluation, ToolPrivacyBench audits whether task-private atoms are routed only to authorized tools and downstream sinks, thereby evaluating both task completion and privacy over-disclosure during tool use. The benchmark contains 2,150 cases, including 1,150 fully synthetic privacy-sensitive business workflows and 1,000 cases adapted from existing multi-tool and function-calling benchmarks. Each case is represented by a policy knowledge base. After an agent executes against mock business backends, the evaluator compares recorded tool arguments and backend audit logs with this policy knowledge base. The evaluation covers nine widely used agents to characterize purpose-bound privacy over-disclosure. The results show that successful tool execution does not imply appropriate privacy disclosure: an agent may complete a task while transmitting unnecessary private information through intermediate tool calls. ToolPrivacyBench therefore formalizes a need-to-know disclosure boundary, under which each tool should receive only the information necessary for its stated purpose, and uses trajectory-level auditing to identify privacy over-disclosure in multi-tool workflows.

</details>


### [42] [GTI-mSEMP Framework : A Proposed Framework to Simulate Malware Propagation with Inclusion of Attacker-Defender Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28079)
*Shadeeb Hossain,Kristopher Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种博弈论集成的改进型多无线传感器流行病恶意软件传播（GTI-mSEMP）框架，用于分析异构、资源受限的网络中攻防双方的动态博弈过程，并通过数值仿真揭示了不同攻防态势下的流行病传播曲线变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病模型将安全防御视为静态参数，无法捕捉攻防双方之间的策略性、非对称博弈行为，难以应对自动化、多向量恶意软件威胁对异构资源受限网络带来的风险。

Method: 将博弈论与改进的多无线传感器流行病恶意软件传播模型（mSEMP）相结合，分析易感（S）和恢复（R）节点群体在三种不同运行状态（平衡对抗、攻击爆发、强化防御）下的轨迹变化，并通过数值仿真验证。

Result: 数值仿真展示了网络状态变量的实时瞬态动力学特征，揭示了当攻防缩放向量存在效率优势时流行病曲线的偏移规律。

Conclusion: 该数学与数值框架为高对抗性网络环境中动态恶意软件传播的评估与局部节点群体状态的预测提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of automated, multi-vector malware threats poses a significant risk to heterogeneous, resource constrained cyber-physical networks. Conventional epidemiological models often treat security defenses as static parameters, failing to capture the strategic, asymmetric maneuvers between an attacker and a defender. To address the gap, this paper proposes a Game-Theory-Integrated Modified Multi- Wireless Sensor Epidemic Malware Propagation (GTI-mSEMP) framework. This paper analyzed and compared the operational trajectories of Susceptible (S) and Recovered (R) node populations across three different operational regimes: Balanced Matchup, Exploit Surge and Hardened Defense. Numerical simulation results capture the real-time transient dynamics of the network state variables, demonstrating how the epidemic curve shifts when either the defensive or offensive scaling vectors hold an efficiency advantage. The proposed mathematical and numerical framework provides a rigorous foundation that can be deployed in highly adversarial network environments to evaluate dynamic malware propagation and predict localized node population states.

</details>


### [43] [Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28153)
*Yanchen Yin,Dongqi Han,Linghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现越狱攻击并非完全消除LLM的安全对齐，而是选择性抑制特定注意力头（ACHs），同时安全对齐头（SAHs）仍维持稳健激活。基于此发现，无需训练直接读取这些持久激活即可实现具有强对抗鲁棒性的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越狱攻击能够绕过LLM的安全对齐，但其内在机制尚不明确，理解这一机制对于构建更安全的AI系统至关重要。

Method: 通过分析注意力头在越狱攻击下的功能分化，识别出两类注意力头：早期层的Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs)在攻击下被抑制，中层Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs)保持稳健激活。通过消融实验验证其因果关系，并进行token级归因分析。

Result: 抑制少量ACHs即足以在正常被拒输入上诱发类越狱行为；去除SAHs会显著削弱中层安全激活。ACH抑制由攻击模板token驱动，揭示了攻击通过ACH抑制绕过拒绝决策但SAHs仍维持内部安全信号的机制（Robust Harmful Features）。无需训练直接读取这些持久激活即可获得具有强对抗鲁棒性的检测性能。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击并非完全消除安全特征，而是选择性抑制特定注意力头；SAHs维持的稳健安全激活可用于实用的对抗鲁棒检测。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.

</details>

### [44] [Kimball's Data Warehouse Architecture: Evaluating the Challenges of Conformed Data against the Inmon Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27571)
*Júlio Rocha,Salviano Soares,António Valente,Filipe Cabral Pinto*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文比较了数据仓库领域两种经典架构——Inmon的企业信息工厂（中心辐射型）与Kimball的数据总线架构（一致性维度），并从信息质量、系统质量、个体影响和组织影响四个维度进行对比分析。尽管两者方法不同，但并非对立关系，Kimball甚至在其著作中引用了Inmon的推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据快速增长，组织需要重构存储框架以高效处理企业应用中的查询需求，因此有必要比较Inmon和Kimball两种主流数据仓库架构的差异与适用性。

Method: 通过文献回顾，对比Inmon的"中心辐射型"架构与Kimball的"数据总线+一致性维度"架构，并基于Ariyachandra和Watson提出的四个维度（信息质量、系统质量、个体影响、组织影响）进行系统比较。

Result: 两种架构在信息质量、系统质量、个体影响和组织影响四个维度上存在显著差异：Inmon架构自上而下、强调企业级数据集成与规范化；Kimball架构自下而上、以用户需求为中心、采用星型模型和一致性维度。

Conclusion: Inmon与Kimball的架构虽方法不同但非对立关系，组织应根据自身需求选择合适的架构，或结合两者优势进行混合部署。

Abstract: In recent decades, driven by rapid data growth, organisations have faced the need to restructure their storage frameworks to efficiently handle queries requested by employees through available enterprise applications. Investigating this need involves examining the classic approaches of William H. Inmon, widely known as the father of Data Warehousing, and Ralph Kimball. Although both shared the same core concerns, Kimball later suggested an alternative architecture focused primarily on user needs. According to Ariyachandra and Watson, Inmon's "hub-and-spoke" architecture and Kimball's data bus framework featuring conformed dimensions stand out among alternative approaches. A comparison across these architectures highlights four key aspects: Information Quality, System Quality, Individual Impacts, and Organisational Impacts. Although Kimball and Inmon proposed contrasting solutions, they did not view each other as rivals. For instance, in one of the editions of the book "The Data Warehouse Toolkit", published by Kimball in 1996, the back cover features a note by Inmon stating that it is "one of the definitive books of our industry. If you take time to read only one professional book, make it this book.

</details>


### [45] [DiStash: A Disaggregated Multi-Stash Transactional Key-Value Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27979)
*Yiming Gao,Hieu Nguyen,Jun Li,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DiStash是一个跨异构存储池（DRAM、SSD、HDD、NVM）的松耦合事务型KV存储系统，支持单一事务跨存储池读写多副本KV对，通过FoundationDB扩展实现，并在eBay生产负载下验证了设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用常使用多种存储介质（DRAM、SSD、HDD、NVM），但缺乏一个统一的事务机制来管理跨不同存储池的数据一致性和故障恢复，导致应用逻辑复杂且容易产生竞态条件或数据丢失。

Method: 基于FoundationDB扩展实现DiStash，支持应用配置存储池为临时或持久存储，并选择包容（复制）或排他（分层）策略。通过单一事务接口实现跨存储池的KV对读写，避免竞态条件和故障导致的数据不一致。

Result: 通过微基准测试和eBay生产负载量化了设计决策的权衡，并在GitHub开源了实现。

Conclusion: DiStash通过松耦合的事务机制简化了跨异构存储池的应用逻辑，提供了灵活的数据一致性和持久性保证，且在实际生产负载下验证了其有效性。

Abstract: A stash is a storage medium such as Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM), Solid State Disk (SSD), Hard Disk Drive (HDD), or Non-Volatile Memory (NVM). This paper presents a disaggregated transactional key-value (KV) store, DiStash, that governs KVs cross pools of stash types. It enables an application to use a single transaction to read and write different copies of one or more key-value pair across the different pools of stashes. It simplifies the application logic by (a) preventing undesirable race conditions that may cause copies of data across different stash pools to reflect different values and/or (b) failures that may result in loss of key-value pairs. A configuration of DiStash may use a pool of stashes as either ephemeral or durable storage. The application dictates whether the content of its participating stashes are inclusive (replicated) or exclusive (tiered). We implement a DiStash by extending FoundationDB. We quantify the tradeoffs with its design decisions using microbenchmarks and eBay's production workload. We open source our implementation at https://github.com/ebay-USC/DiStash.

</details>


### [46] [Single and Multi Truth Data Fusion using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28062)
*Hira Beril Kucuk,Norman W Paton,Jiaoyan Chen,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLMs）进行表格数据融合（真相发现）任务，实验表明LLM方法在单真和多真场景下均优于传统无监督方法（如DART和LTM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据融合（真相发现）是数据集成中的关键问题，传统无监督方法在多源冲突数据中确定正确值的能力有限，本文旨在探索LLM在该任务上的潜力。

Method: 针对单真和多真两种场景，设计了多种提示策略（包括领域相关/无关、零样本/单样本），在三个基准数据集上进行实证评估。

Result: LLM方法在所有数据集上均优于DART和LTM等传统无监督真相发现方法。

Conclusion: LLM在表格数据融合任务中具有显著优势，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Data fusion, also known as truth discovery, is a data integration problem that aims to determine the correct value or set of values for each attribute of an object when presented with potentially conflicting values from multiple sources. Data fusion tasks belong to two main categories: single-truth scenarios, where each attribute has only one correct value, and multi-truth scenarios, where multiple values can be valid simultaneously. This paper investigates the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in data fusion tasks for tabular data. Various prompting strategies, encompassing both single-truth and multi-truth scenarios, are investigated empirically. Domain-dependent, domain-independent, zero-shot and one-shot prompts are evaluated on three different benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that LLM-based approaches outperform traditional unsupervised truth discovery methods, such as DART and LTM, across all datasets. The codebase of this study has been made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>

### [47] [Support-Constrained RL Enables Real-World Policy Improvement without Real-World Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27475)
*Raymond Yu,William Huey,Mustafa Mukadam,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCORE框架，通过流约束策略优化，将真实世界数据预训练的策略在仿真中通过支持约束的强化学习进行改进，在8项灵巧操作任务中将平均成功率从37.8%提升至89.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界训练的机器人策略不精确、缓慢且脆弱；强化学习改进需昂贵真实训练；仿真中无约束RL因接触/动力学不匹配导致不安全行为且无法迁移；现有正则化方法过度约束导致改进有限。

Method: 提出Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE)，通过流约束（flow steering）将仿真中的RL限制在预训练生成策略的支持集内，确保动作可迁移同时最大化策略改进。学习稀疏奖励，无需蒸馏，不修改基础策略。

Result: 在8项真实世界灵巧多指机器人操作任务中，SCORE将平均成功率从37.8%提升至89.9%，最佳基线为59.5%；同时比基础策略减少36.8%的步数。

Conclusion: 当策略优化被适当约束时，仿真可以显著提升真实世界操作策略，提出了真实到仿真再到真实策略改进的新范式。

Abstract: Robots trained on real world data tend to be imprecise, slow, and brittle to perturbations. Improving these policies with reinforcement learning (RL) is an appealing alternative, but this process often requires expensive training in the real world. Performing policy improvement in simulation instead provides a far cheaper alternative, but unconstrained RL in simulation can exploit contact and dynamics mismatches, resulting in unsafe behaviors that do not transfer to hardware. Common forms of regularization can furthermore limit improvement by overconstraining to an imperfect behavior prior. In this work, we propose Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE), a real-to-sim-to-real framework that constrains RL in simulation to the support of a generative policy pretrained on real data. We instantiate this constraint through flow steering, restricting SCORE to actions the base policy can already produce, which ensures transferable behaviors while maximizing policy improvement. Improving a policy with SCORE requires minimal effort: it learns from sparse rewards, avoids distillation, and leaves the base policy untouched. Across eight real-world dexterous multi-fingered robotic manipulation tasks, SCORE improves average success rate from 37.8% to 89.9%, compared to 59.5% for the best baseline, and reaches success in 36.8% fewer steps than the base policy. Ultimately, through extensive experiments and ablations, we show that simulation can substantially improve real-world manipulation policies when policy optimization is appropriately constrained, introducing a new paradigm for real-to-sim-to-real policy improvement. Videos and code are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/score/.

</details>


### [48] [AO-ARC: Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Motion Planning with ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27495)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AO-ARC是一种将可行性求解器转化为任意时间算法的多机器人运动规划方法，通过迭代调用ARC方法在有界makespan成本度量下，实现与最先进可行性求解器相当的初始解速度，并在机器人数量增加时比现有任意时间方法更快更可靠地收敛。论文提供了渐近最优性的理论证明，并在2D和3D场景中进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多机器人运动规划（MRMP）可行性求解器虽然能快速找到初始解，但缺乏解的质量保证；而现有的任意时间MRMP方法在机器人数量增加时收敛速度慢且不可靠。需要一种既能快速获得初始解又能高效收敛到高质量解的方法。

Method: AO-ARC将AO-x元算法应用于ARC可行性求解器，通过在有界makespan成本度量下迭代调用ARC方法处理有界MRMP实例，利用ARC的自适应（解）耦合特性，同时保持AO-x所需的跨机器人（解）组成的一致成本边界。

Result: 在2D场景（不同协调复杂度）和3D机械臂场景中，AO-ARC的初始解速度与最先进的MRMP可行性求解器相当，且在机器人数量增加时收敛速度更快、更可靠。

Conclusion: AO-ARC成功结合了可行性求解器的快速初始解能力和任意时间算法的渐进最优性，理论证明了其渐近最优性，实证验证了其在高维、多机器人场景下的高效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present AO-ARC, an anytime multi-robot motion planning (MRMP) method that achieves initial solution times on par with state-of-the-art MRMP feasibility solvers while converging faster and more reliably than existing anytime MRMP methods as the number of robots increases. AO-ARC adapts the AO-x meta-algorithm for converting feasibility solvers into anytime algorithms by iteratively calling the original ARC method on bounded MRMP instances under a makespan cost metric. This exploits the adaptive (de)coupling of ARC while maintaining the consistent cost bound across robot (de)compositions needed for AO-x. We provide theoretical analysis proving the asymptotic optimality properties of AO- ARC and conduct empirical evaluation on a set of 2D scenarios with different levels of coordination complexity and a 3D manipulator scenario representative of real-world applications.

</details>


### [49] [Drifting in the Future: Stabilizing Path Following Drifting on High-Latency Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27914)
*Frederik Werner,Till Heintzenberg,Markus Lienkamp,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种控制器框架，通过设计预测器补偿动力总成延迟、改进控制公式适应高执行器延迟和差速耦合、引入基于制动的速度稳定，成功在量产跑车上实现了稳健的圆周和八字漂移，横向误差1.1米，侧偏角超调0.06弧度，证明了量产车自主漂移的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的自动漂移研究局限于具有瞬时扭矩传递和独立驱动轮的研究平台，不适用于存在执行器延迟和机械耦合车桥的量产车辆。本文旨在解决量产车在稳定性极限下的自主控制问题。

Method: 1) 设计预测器补偿动力总成延迟；2) 开发改进的控制公式以适应更高执行器延迟及驱动桥差速耦合；3) 引入基于制动的速度稳定策略。

Result: 量产跑车可稳健维持圆周和八字漂移，横向误差1.1米，侧偏角超调0.06弧度，尽管执行器延迟超过250毫秒，仍能抑制振荡并保持稳定的路径和侧偏角跟踪。

Conclusion: 量产车实现自主漂移可行，为在传统控制失效场景下能够稳定车辆的高级安全系统开辟了道路。

Abstract: Autonomously controlling and handling a vehicle at and beyond its stability limit is a mathematically and computationally demanding task. Prior demonstrations of automated drifting have been limited to research platforms with instantaneous torque delivery and independently actuated wheels, leaving their applicability to production vehicles with actuator latencies and mechanically coupled axles uncertain. To overcome these issues, we design a predictor to compensate for powertrain delays, develop a revised control formulation to accommodate higher actuation latencies as well as a differential coupling on the driven axle, and introduce brake-based velocity stabilization. This paper presents the controller framework, the model extensions, and real-world experimental results. We observe that our controller enables a production sports car with a combustion engine to robustly sustain circular and figure-eight drifts, limiting lateral error to 1.1 m and sideslip overshoot to 0.06 rad despite actuator delays exceeding 250 ms, while mitigating oscillations and maintaining stable path and sideslip tracking. In conclusion, our results establish that autonomous drifting is feasible on production-ready vehicles, opening pathways to advanced safety systems capable of stabilizing cars in scenarios where traditional control fails.

</details>


### [50] [Spacecraft Fiducial Marker for Autonomous Rendezvous, Proximity Operations, and Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27566)
*Ravi Kumar Thakur,Matouš Vrba,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AstraTag，一种基于方形Spidron递归自相似结构的 fiducial marker，支持多尺度检测，并采用48位GRS编码签名和TPS重映射弯面校正，在曲面航天器模型上检测率优于Fractal ArUco和AprilTag。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 fiducial marker 多为单尺度且针对地面机器人设计，在空间机器人的近距离交会对接阶段会离开相机视野，无法可靠追踪。

Method: 采用方形Spidron图案实现递归自相似多尺度检测；使用48位签名（基于模板三角子区域）结合Generalised Reed-Solomon (GRS) 码编码；检测流程含基于轮廓的四边形定位、透视归一化及预计算字典签名匹配；引入Thin-Plate Spline (TPS) 重映射降级策略，利用内部矩形边界作为额外几何对应点处理曲面形变。

Result: 在平面和曲面航天器模型上，AstraTag在曲面上的检测率优于三层Fractal ArUco和AprilTag两个基线方法。

Conclusion: AstraTag为空间机器人提供了一种鲁棒的递归式多尺度 markers 方案，尤其适合曲面安装场景。

Abstract: Robotic operations in space are challenging due to the harsh environment and the high cost of failure. Fiducial markers provide visual references that aid autonomous rendezvous, proximity operations, and docking for space robots. However, existing fiducial markers are mostly single-scale and largely designed for terrestrial robotics. Such markers leave the camera's field of view at close range, precisely during the proximity and docking phases where reliable tracking is most critical. This paper presents AstraTag, a fiducial marker designed for autonomous on-orbit robotic operations. The marker template is based on a square Spidron pattern whose recursive, self-similar structure enables detection across multiple spatial scales. Marker identification uses a 48-bit signature derived from triangular sub-regions of the template and encoded with a Generalised Reed-Solomon (GRS) code. The detection pipeline performs contour-based quadrilateral localisation, perspective normalisation, and signature matching against a pre-computed dictionary. To handle markers affixed to curved spacecraft surfaces, it incorporates a Thin-Plate Spline (TPS) re-warp fallback that exploits the marker's internal rectangular borders as additional geometric correspondences. We benchmark AstraTag against three-layer Fractal ArUco and AprilTag on spacecraft mockups with flat and curved surfaces. On curved surfaces, AstraTag achieves a higher detection rate than both baselines, offering a robust recursive-marker option for space robotics.

</details>


### [51] [SceneBot: Contact-Prompted General Humanoid Whole Body Tracking with Scene-Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27581)
*Sirui Chen,Shibo Zhao,Zhen Wu,Jiaman Li,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneBot是一个统一的运动跟踪框架，通过将策略同时基于参考动作和每链路接触标签，能够处理自由空间运动、地形穿越和全身操作，并在7.5小时的重建接触丰富数据上训练后成功泛化到未见动作和环境，首次实现了自由空间与接触丰富行为的无缝统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人强化学习策略在自由空间运动中表现出色，但在接触丰富任务（如与物体交互、在不平坦地形上行走）中表现不佳，因为纯运动学跟踪无法解决物理交互中的模糊性问题。

Method: 提出SceneBot框架，将单个策略同时条件化于参考动作和每链路接触标签，显式定义期望的环境交互。为解决缺乏标注交互数据的问题，提出事后场景重建方法，从重定向的人类动作中推断场景交互图。

Result: 在7.5小时的重建接触丰富数据上训练后，SceneBot成功泛化到未见动作和环境，能够执行抱箱子上楼等复杂长时域任务，成为首个统一自由空间与接触丰富行为的通用框架。

Conclusion: SceneBot证明了接触条件化作为人形机器人控制的有效接口，实现了自由空间运动与接触丰富行为的无缝统一，所有代码和数据将开源。

Abstract: Current humanoid reinforcement-learning policies excel at free-space motions but struggle with contact-rich tasks, as pure kinematic tracking cannot resolve the physical ambiguities of interacting with objects and uneven terrain. To address this, we introduce SceneBot, a unified motion-tracking framework capable of handling freespace locomotion, terrain traversal, and whole-body manipulation. SceneBot conditions a single policy on both reference motions and per-link contact labels, explicitly defining expected environmental interactions. To overcome the lack of annotated interaction data, we propose a hindsight scene reconstruction approach that infers scene-interaction graphs from retargeted human motion. Trained on 7.5 hours of this reconstructed, contact-rich data, SceneBot successfully generalizes to unseen motions and environments. Our results demonstrate that SceneBot is the first general framework to seamlessly unify free-space and contact-rich behaviors executing complex, long-horizon tasks like carrying a box upstairs and establishing contact conditioning as a powerful interface for humanoid control. All code and data will be open-sourced. More demos and information are available at: https://ericcsr.github.io/scenebot/

</details>


### [52] [Learning to Throw: Agile and Accurate Cable-Suspended Payload Delivery with a Quadrotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27603)
*Yifan Zhai,Elia Raimondi,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yannick Armati,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合仿真框架，将高保真四旋翼模型与物理求解器耦合以模拟绳吊负载系统，通过深度强化学习训练策略实现精确投掷，在硬件上零样本部署后比模型基线方法降低50%着陆误差和30%投掷时间，并开源仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖基于模型的轨迹优化与跟踪，但受限于保守的可行性约束、跟踪误差以及柔性绳索动力学的解析建模困难，导致性能次优。动态负载释放这一高度动态的关键任务未被充分探索。

Method: 构建混合仿真框架：将高保真四旋翼解析模型与处理复杂绳索-负载交互的物理求解器在每个时间步进行力交换，获得物理精确的仿真环境；在此环境中训练深度强化学习策略以执行敏捷精准的负载投掷。

Result: 零样本部署于真实硬件，相比基于模型的基线方法，着陆误差降低高达50%，投掷持续时间缩短高达30%。消融实验证实耦合仿真是性能提升的关键。基于视觉观测的策略达到与基于状态策略相当的精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了敏捷性与精度之间的权衡边界，为动态空中操作提供了高效的解决方案，开源仿真器将促进该领域的后续研究。

Abstract: Quadrotors offer the agility needed to rapidly transport suspended payloads during time-critical applications, including search-and-rescue and medical delivery. While suspended-payload transport and traversal for these missions are well studied, the highly dynamic targeted release of the payload remains comparatively underexplored. State-of-the-art approaches typically rely on model-based trajectory optimization and tracking; however, these methods often yield sub-optimal performance due to conservative feasibility constraints, tracking errors, and the inherent difficulty of analytically modeling flexible rope dynamics. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid simulation framework that couples a high-fidelity analytical quadrotor model with a physics solver for complex rope and payload interactions. By exchanging forces between the two domains at every step, we obtain a physically accurate simulation of the suspended-payload system. Leveraging this environment, we train a deep reinforcement learning (RL) policy that executes agile, accurate payload throws to designated targets. Deployed zero-shot on hardware, our RL policy pushes the boundary of the agility-accuracy trade-off, outperforming the model-based baseline by reducing the landing error by up to 50% and the throw duration by up to 30%. Ablation studies confirm that the coupled simulation is the key enabler of these gains. We further show that the same pipeline trains a policy driven by visual observations rather than an explicit state estimate, achieving accuracy comparable to that of the state-based policy. To accelerate future research in dynamic aerial manipulation, we open-source the simulator to the community upon acceptance.

</details>


### [53] [Physics-Guided Robotic Radiation Source Localization along Arbitrary Measurement Paths in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27624)
*Hojoon Son,Kai Tan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息机器学习(PIML)的机器人辐射源定位自动化框架，通过设计物理启发的模型张量处理未知障碍物的衰减伽马射线通量信号，并采用并行多模型计算提升鲁棒性和精度。该方法无需机器人逼近辐射源即可实现精确定位，降低了辐射损伤风险，并在高保真蒙特卡洛仿真和真实实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人辐射源定位方法通常需要机器人逼近辐射源以精确估计位置，这增加了机器人的辐射损伤风险；同时专为定位设计的路径规划算法限制了机器人在放射性环境中的任务灵活性。

Method: 提出基于物理信息机器学习(PIML)的自动化框架，设计物理启发的模型张量处理未知障碍物衰减的伽马射线通量信号；采用并行多模型计算提升鲁棒性和精度；结合持续学习技术增强在线部署的实用性。

Result: 在高保真蒙特卡洛粒子传输仿真环境（涵盖多种空间尺度、辐射源类型、障碍物材料和几何形状、机器人轨迹）中进行了评估，并在仿真未包含的配置上进行了物理实验验证。

Conclusion: 该方法将机器人的辐射感知从点状通量检测提升为空间智能，在无需特定路径规划的前提下实现精确辐射源定位，降低了辐射风险并提升了任务灵活性。

Abstract: Using robots to estimate the location of the radiation source is an effective way to improve efficiency and safety. Existing methods focus on planning the robot's path to achieve precise estimation, typically approaching the source. However, approaching the source increases the risk of radiation damage to a robot. In addition, a path-planning algorithm designed solely for radiation source localization (RSL) limits the flexibility of missions that deploy robots into radioactive environments. This study presents an automation framework for robotic RSL that leverages a physics-informed machine learning (PIML) model to precisely estimate the source location, regardless of measurement paths, in unknown environments. Physics-inspired model tensors have been designed for PIML to handle attenuated gamma-ray flux signals from unknown obstacles, and multiple models are computed in parallel to improve the robustness and precision of the RSL. The proposed method is evaluated in high-fidelity simulation environments using Monte Carlo particle transport across diverse randomized domains, including spatial scales, radiation source types, obstacle materials and geometries, and robot trajectories. The method is also validated through physical experiments on configurations that are not included in the simulation-based evaluation. The continuous learning technique is applied in real-robot deployment to enhance the practical applicability of the online robotic RSL system. The proposed method advances robot radiation perception from pointwise flux detection to spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [54] [P-ARC: Exploiting Subproblem Independence for Parallel Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27625)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出P-ARC，一种自适应机器人协调（ARC）方法的并行变体，用于多机器人运动规划。P-ARC在ARC的三个主要阶段（初始个体解、冲突检测、冲突解决）分别设计了并行变体，并采用OR并行多启动策略形成混合并行策略OR-P-ARC。在2D移动机器人和平面机械臂场景（最多128个机器人）以及真实场景中多达16个CPU核上，实现了接近4倍的规划时间加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升ARC多机器人运动规划方法的计算效率，通过并行化解决大规模机器人团队的规划耗时问题。

Method: 1) 对ARC三个阶段分别设计并行变体；2) 利用ARC问题分解带来的独立性；3) 引入OR并行多启动策略，形成混合并行策略OR-P-ARC。

Result: 在2D移动机器人和平面机械臂场景中（最多128个机器人）验证了并行策略的效果；在16个CPU核上，针对Panda多机械臂团队实现了接近4倍的规划时间加速比。

Conclusion: P-ARC和OR-P-ARC能够有效利用并行计算资源，显著加速大规模多机器人运动规划，且加速比接近线性。

Abstract: This paper presents Parallel ARC (P-ARC), a parallel variant of the Adaptive Robot Coordination (ARC) approach to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). P-ARC proposes a parallel variant for each of the three main stages in ARC: initial individual solutions, conflict detection, and conflict resolution, exploiting the independence created by ARC's decomposition of the MRMP problem. Additionally, we employ an OR-parallel multi-start strategy to both ARC and P-ARC, creating a hybrid parallel strategy OR-P-ARC. We evaluate the impact of the different parallel strategies for ARC using a set of scaling 2D mobile and planar manipulator scenarios with up to 128 robots to control for conflicts and work distribution across the stages of ARC. Additionally, we demonstrate planning time speedups approaching 4X over the sequential version for large Panda multi-manipulator teams in real-world inspired scenarios when deploying 16 CPU cores.

</details>


### [55] [Direct Action-Head Injection of A Grounded 3D Point Unlocks Spatial and Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27663)
*Shiang-Feng Tsai,Jin-Cheng Jhang,Yen-Ling Tai,Jia-Hong Lai,Shih-Yun Wong,KangTung-Hsu,Yi-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级、模型无关的模块，通过将接地信号表示为3D点云并计算其与夹爪的相对位移，再通过自适应层归一化将空间嵌入注入动作头，显著提升了VLA模型在空间泛化和任务泛化上的表现。在LIBERO-PRO上，GR00T-N1.6在任务扰动下成功率从31.2提升至77.5，位置扰动下从28.1提升至60.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在测试时对物体位置变化（空间泛化）和指令变化（任务泛化）表现脆弱，虽然已有方法通过2D像素坐标提供空间和任务信息，但效果有限。本文发现：关键在于如何表示和注入接地信号——采用3D点云表示并直接注入动作头才是真正的突破点。

Method: 提出一个轻量级、模型无关的模块：(1) 将接地信号表示为3D点云；(2) 计算其与夹爪的相对位移；(3) 通过自适应层归一化将空间嵌入直接注入动作头。整个模块仅为一个两层MLP，无需修改VLA主干网络或预训练流程。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上，GR00T-N1.6在任务扰动下成功率从31.2提升至77.5（+46.3），位置扰动下从28.1提升至60.2（+32.1）。在π₀.₅上也取得可比增益，验证了方法的骨干网络无关性。

Conclusion: 充分的3D接地信号直接注入动作头，是解锁VLA空间与任务泛化的关键，且仅需在预训练骨干网络上增加一个轻量级模块即可实现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for flexible robot manipulation, yet at test time they remain brittle along two axes: spatial generalization, when object positions differ from those seen during training, and task generalization, when a familiar scene is paired with a different language instruction than the one seen in training. A growing family of methods addresses this brittleness by endowing a policy with the spatial and task-aware information such as 2D pixel-coordinate for object localization and placement. However, we find that existing representation through language prompting or visual prompting does not address the limitations; in contrast, exploiting a 3D point-based representation and feeding it directly to the action head leads to substantial improvements-revealing that how the grounding signal is represented and injected into the VLA is the true game changer. Thus, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic module that represents the grounding signal in 3D, computes its relative displacement to the gripper, and injects the resulting spatial embedding directly into the action head through adaptive layer normalization. The entire module is a two-layer MLP that requires no changes to the VLA backbone or pretraining pipeline. On LIBERO-PRO, our method improves the average success rate of GR00T-N1.6 from 31.2 to 77.5 points under task perturbation and from 28.1 to 60.2 points under position perturbation (gains of 46.3 and 32.1 points). Comparable gains are achieved for $π_{0.5}$ as well, demonstrating that the mechanism is backbone-agnostic. Together, these results support our central finding: given adequate grounding lifted into 3D, injecting it directly into the action head is what unlocks both spatial and task generalization in VLAs-achievable with nothing more than a lightweight module on top of a pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [56] [CWI: Composite Humanoid Whole-Body Imitation System for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27676)
*Wenqi Ge,Junde Guo,Zhen Fu,Shunpeng Yang,Jiayu Chen,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI)框架，通过解耦上半身操作和下半身运动的动作捕捉数据使用，结合AMP和multi-critic架构，实现人形机器人的全身协调控制，在仿真和真实LimX Oli机器人上验证了竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制器面临两难困境：纯命令采样训练方法因稀疏奖励难以收敛且动作偏离类人特征；而模仿全身MoCap数据又面临数据集不平衡问题（过多激进运动轨迹），需要大量数据过滤和增强。

Method: CWI框架解耦MoCap数据使用：上半身利用完整操作参考MoCap数据集，下半身通过双重判别器（基于AMP）在精选行走/蹲起片段上训练实现稳定、命令调节的 locomotion；采用多评论家架构减少 locomotion、操作和运动风格目标间的冲突；通过教师-学生蒸馏阶段得到仅需双手姿态和速度/高度命令的全身策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实LimX Oli人形机器人上展示出竞争力的locomotion-manipulation性能、鲁棒的全身协调性以及无需全身动作捕捉设备的实用遥操作能力。

Conclusion: CWI通过创新性地解耦上下身运动数据利用策略，有效解决了人形机器人全身控制中数据利用与运动协调的难题，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving everyday tasks with humanoid robots requires coordinating stable locomotion with versatile manipulation. However, existing whole-body controllers still face significant challenges. Methods trained solely via command sampling, without motion-capture (MoCap) data, often struggle with sparse rewards and require carefully tuned curricula to converge. This is especially problematic for upper-body control, where the resulting motions deviate from human-like statistics and degrade whole-body coordination. Conversely, approaches that imitate full-body MoCap data suffer from dataset imbalance, as many locomotion trajectories are overly aggressive for stable-locomotion scenarios, necessitating extensive data filtering and augmentation. To address this, we present Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI), a framework that decouples the use of MoCap data for upper-body manipulation and lower-body locomotion. This decoupling allows us to exploit the full MoCap dataset of diverse manipulation references, while stable, command-conditioned lower-body locomotion is guided by dual discriminators trained on curated expert-quality walking and squatting clips via an Adversarial Motion Prior (AMP). A multi-critic architecture reduces conflicts among locomotion, manipulation, and motion-style objectives, and a teacher--student distillation stage yields a whole-body policy conditioned only on bimanual hand poses and velocity/height commands. We evaluate CWI through simulation experiments and real-world deployment on a full-size LimX Oli humanoid. The results show competitive loco-manipulation performance, robust whole-body coordination, and practical teleoperation without full-body motion-capture equipment. A project page with supplementary material can be found at https://cwi-ral.github.io/CWI-RAL-Webpage.

</details>


### [57] [DIM-WAM: World-Action Modeling with Diverse Historical Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27677)
*Kai Wang,Zhaopeng Gu,Yixiang Chen,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Peng Su,Zhaowen Li,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiM-WAM，一种基于记忆增强的世界-动作模型，通过多尺度历史上下文、局部未来动态和全局任务进度预测，解决了长期任务中遗忘和任务状态感知不足的问题。在RMBench基准上将平均成功率从28.4%提升至69.8%，在真实Franka任务上全任务成功率从52.5%提升至80.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型主要依赖短期历史与短视距未来预测，无法有效利用长期依赖信息（如近期局部上下文、跨阶段历史事件、即时未来动态以及全局任务进度），导致在长时程任务中出现长期遗忘和对全局任务状态感知不足的问题。

Method: DiM-WAM构建了记忆增强的世界-动作模型，核心包括：1）从真实观测中提取紧凑的视觉事件信息；2）通过独立的基于相似性的合并机制更新多个记忆库；3）读取带有库标识和时间编码的长期上下文，以条件化视频和动作去噪过程；4）引入进度监督目标，促使记忆token不仅编码已完成的历史事件，还编码当前任务阶段及其对剩余任务的含义。

Result: 在RMBench上，平均成功率从LingBot-VA的28.4%提升至69.8%，超过显式记忆基线Mem-0（42.0%）；在四个真实世界Franka任务上，阶段成功率从70.7%提升至91.5%，全任务成功率从52.5%提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: DiM-WAM通过多尺度记忆增强和进度监督，有效解决了长时程机器人操作任务中的长期依赖和任务状态感知问题，显著提升了任务成功率，验证了记忆增强世界-动作模型在长时程任务中的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models have shown promising robot-manipulation performance by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. However, existing methods mainly rely on short-term history and short-horizon future prediction, which is insufficient for long-horizon tasks whose correct execution depends on earlier observations and task progress. Such temporally dependent tasks require effective use of complementary temporal information, including recent local context, cross-stage historical events, immediate future dynamics, and global task progress. To address long-term forgetting and poor awareness of the global task state, we introduce DiM-WAM, a memory-augmented world-action model that integrates multi-scale historical context, local future dynamics, and global task progress. The memory extracts compact visual event information from real observations, updates multiple memory banks through independent similarity-based merging, and then reads the bank-identity- and time-embedded long-term context to condition video and action denoising. A progress-supervision objective further encourages memory tokens to encode not only completed historical events but also the current task stage and its implications for the remaining task. On RMBench, DiM-WAM raises average success from 28.4% with LingBot-VA to 69.8%, exceeding the explicit-memory Mem-0 baseline at 42.0%. On four real-world Franka tasks, it improves average stage success from 70.7% to 91.5% and full-task success from 52.5% to 80.0%. Project page: https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/{\texttt{https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/}}.

</details>


### [58] [Drop-Then-Recovery: How Redundant Are Vision-Language-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27755)
*Guoheng Sun,Kaixi Feng,Shwai He,Xiaochuan Gong,Yexiao He,Ziyao Wang,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)分析协议和GateProbe灵敏度指标，系统研究VLA模型中的架构冗余性。实验发现语言主干在标准机器人操作任务中高度冗余，而视觉和动作通路则不可或缺。在LIBERO基准上，移除一半LLM模块甚至使OpenVLA-OFT的性能从95.0%提升至98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型继承了预训练VLM中过大的语言骨干网络，其容量远超机器人简短指令控制的实际需求。作者质疑当前VLA模型中存在大量架构冗余，试图回答一个基本问题：对于闭环控制，VLA模型到底有多大比例是真正必要的？

Method: 提出DTR协议：先移除预训练VLA模型中的选定Transformer模块，再对剩余模型进行微调，观察性能恢复情况来判定被移除容量是否必要。同时提出GateProbe指标，通过一次性虚拟门控机制计算各模块对下游动作损失的贡献灵敏度，用于排序模块重要性。

Result: 语言骨干高度冗余，移除一半LLM模块在LIBERO上性能从95.0%提升至98.3%；仅保留两个语言模块仍可恢复基线性能。视觉和动作通路对移除的容忍度显著更低。

Conclusion: 当前VLA基准对深度语言理解和组合指令指令施加的压力有限；未来VLA架构应在语言、视觉、动作组件之间更审慎地分配容量。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable instruction-driven robotic manipulation, but they inherit oversized language backbones from pretrained VLMs whose capacity far exceeds what is needed for short robotic instructions. This raises a basic question: how much of a VLA model is actually necessary for closed-loop control? In this work, we study architectural redundancy in VLA models by using transformer block removal as a controlled intervention. We introduce \textbf{Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)}, an analysis protocol that removes selected blocks from a pretrained VLA model and then fine-tunes the resulting model to measure whether the removed capacity was necessary for downstream control. To make this intervention reliable, we propose \textbf{GateProbe}, a one-shot virtual-gate sensitivity metric that ranks blocks by their contribution to the downstream action loss. Across multiple VLA architectures, manipulation benchmarks and even real-robot industrial scenarios, we find a strong asymmetry in post-removal recoverability: \ul{\textit{language backbones are highly redundant for standard robotic manipulation tasks, whereas vision and action pathways are substantially less tolerant to removal}}. On LIBERO, removing half of the LLM blocks even improves OpenVLA-OFT from 95.0% to 98.3% under the same downstream fine-tuning budget, and retaining only two language blocks still recovers baseline-level performance. These results suggest that current VLA benchmarks may exert limited pressure on deep language grounding and compositional instruction understanding, and that future VLA architectures should allocate capacity more deliberately across language, vision, and action components. The code is available at https://github.com/s1ghhh/VLADrop.

</details>


### [59] [SpikeVLA: Vision-Language-Action Models with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27807)
*Ruiqi Song,Dujun Nie,Siyu Teng,Baiyong Ding,Xiaotong Zhang,Dong Li,Chenming Zhang,Yuchen Li,Hangbin Wu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpikeVLA是一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，通过事件驱动的脉冲编码替代传统连续层，在降低能耗和计算成本的同时保持了具身导航与机器人控制任务的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基于大规模Transformer，存在推理延迟高、能耗大的问题，难以在低功耗、实时场景中实际部署。

Method: 提出SpikeVLA架构，包含三个关键组件：Spike-V（脉冲视觉编码器，用事件驱动脉冲层替代连续层）、Spike-L（多模态脉冲大语言模型，引入脉冲动力学和令牌级事件驱动稀疏性）、Spike-A（脉冲动作策略网络，采用拉普拉斯核群体编码和多层全连接SNN）。

Result: 在导航和机器人控制任务上，SpikeVLA显著降低了能耗和计算成本，同时保持了与现有方法竞争的性能力。

Conclusion: SpikeVLA展示了脉冲神经网络架构在低功耗、实时具身智能应用中的潜力，为VLA模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a dominant paradigm for embodied intelligence. However, most existing approaches are built on large-scale transformers, resulting in substantial inference latency and energy consumption that limit their practical deployment in low-power, real-time scenarios. We propose SpikeVLA, a spiking VLA architecture for embodied navigation with energy-efficient inference, consisting of three key components. (i) A spiking vision encoder, Spike-V, that replaces dense continuous layers with event-driven spiking layers to reduce the energy consumption of visual representation learning. (ii) A multi-modal spiking large language model, Spike-L, that reformulates cross-modal reasoning with spiking dynamics and token-level event-driven sparsity to further lower computational cost. (iii) A spiking action policy network, Spike-A employs Laplacian-kernel population coding with a multi-layer fully connected SNN, and decodes spiking activities into stable and robust continuous control with energy-efficient inference under low-power constraints. Experiments on navigation and robotic control tasks show that SpikeVLA significantly reduces energy consumption and computational cost while maintaining competitive performance, highlighting its potential for low-power, real-time embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [60] [LXD-SLAM: LiDAR+X Dense SLAM with $\sum_{i=0}^{5}C_5^i$ Configurable Sensor Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27811)
*Zhong Wang,Lin Zhang,Linfei Li,Ying Shen,Shaoming Zhang,Pengcheng Shi,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LXD-SLAM是一个以3D LiDAR为核心的统一多传感器融合框架，支持多达32种传感器组合的即插即用集成，采用统一的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波器和连续多层高斯过程子网格建模，实现了高精度、全局一致的实时密集建图和定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统在复杂环境中面临几何退化与传感器漂移问题，多传感器融合缺乏模块化适应性，且存在数学上不一致的融合或次优的地图表示。

Method: 提出LXD-SLAM框架：1) 以3D LiDAR为核心，支持LiDAR、相机、IMU、轮式编码器、GNSS的即插即用，共32种组合；2) 采用统一的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波(I-ESKF)，结合自适应分层预测策略和点-网格距离与视觉重投影误差的最小化更新；3) 使用连续多层高斯过程子网格建模环境，实现高效的射线-网格深度恢复；4) 引入扩展扫描上下文(ESC)描述子和双向PnP优化，实现鲁棒的多模态闭环检测。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实世界实验中，LXD-SLAM在各种配置下匹配或超越了最先进的专用里程计方案，并实时生成高保真、全局一致的密集网格。

Conclusion: LXD-SLAM通过统一的多传感器融合框架和高斯过程子网格表示，有效解决了SLAM中的几何退化与漂移问题，实现了高精度、高模块化、实时性的密集建图和定位。

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is essential for autonomous systems, yet achieving reliable, globally consistent pose estimation and dense mapping in complex environments remains challenging due to geometric degeneracy and sensor drift. While multi-sensor fusion addresses these issues, existing systems often lack the modularity to adapt to diverse platforms and rely on mathematically inconsistent fusion or suboptimal map representations. To address these limitations, we propose LXD-SLAM (LiDAR+X Dense SLAM), a highly versatile and unified multi-sensor fusion framework. Centered around 3D LiDAR, our system allows for the plug-and-play integration of LiDAR, Camera, IMU, Wheel Encoder, and GNSS, supporting up to 32 distinct sensor combinations. We employ a mathematically unified Iterative Error-Sate Kalman Filter with an adaptive hierarchical prediction strategy and an update step that minimizes point-to-mesh distances and visual reprojection errors. To support this, the environment is modeled using continuous multi-layered Gaussian Process (GP) sub-meshes, which enables efficient ray-to-mesh depth recovery for visual features. For global consistency, we introduce an Extended Scan Context (ESC) descriptor derived from the GP sub-meshes alongside a Bidirectional PnP optimization for robust multi-modal loop closure within a hybrid pose graph. Extensive evaluations on public datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate that LXD-SLAM matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized odometry solutions across various configurations while generating high-fidelity, globally consistent dense meshes in real-time. The relevant codes and data will be made available at https://github.com/peterWon/LXD-SLAM upon publication.

</details>


### [61] [Booster Lab: A Data-Centric Pipeline for Learning Deployable Humanoid Locomotion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27813)
*Penghui Chen,Tinglong Zheng,Yufeng Zhang,Mingguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个数据驱动的类人机器人运动学习流水线，涵盖数据筛选、虚实迁移、AMP强化学习及实机部署，并在Booster T1和K1机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可行运动数据稀缺：人类演示与机器人形态不兼容、开源数据质量参差、仿真数据仍需可行性检查，亟需一种高效的数据筛选与训练框架。

Method: 构建数据中心的训练-部署流水线，包括运动数据筛选、真实到仿真模型适配、基于对抗运动先验（AMP）的强化学习训练、以及仿真到实机迁移。

Result: 在Booster T1机器人上完成框架验证，并在Booster K1上进行了初步跨平台验证。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效利用稀缺的机器人可行运动数据，学习到物理可行且自然的类人运动策略，并具备一定的跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robot motion learning requires not only task-oriented control policies but also physically feasible and natural behaviors that can be transferred to real robots. However, robot-feasible motion data are often scarce: raw human demonstrations may be incompatible with the robot morphology, open-source clips vary in quality, and simulation-collected robot trajectories still require feasibility checking. To address these challenges, we propose a data-centric training and deployment pipeline that integrates motion data curation, real-to-sim model adaptation, AMP-based reinforcement learning, and sim-to-real deployment. We validate the framework on the Booster T1 robot and further provide preliminary cross-platform validation on Booster K1.

</details>


### [62] [PPO-EAL: Exact Augmented Lagrangian Proximal Policy Optimization for Safe Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27861)
*Jiatao Ding,Songqun Gao,Andrea Del Prete,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PPO-EAL框架，将精确增广拉格朗日优化集成到PPO中，实现安全强化学习的精确约束满足与高效策略学习，在多项机器人任务中超越现有基线，并实现零样本仿真到现实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法在满足精确物理约束的同时难以高效学习策略，当前算法在约束满足精度和样本效率上存在不足。

Method: 将精确增广拉格朗日优化集成到PPO中，结合裁剪策略更新与精确二次惩罚项，并引入动量正则化的乘子更新机制以提高对偶变量稳定性。

Result: 在车杆平衡、双摆稳定、7自由度Franka机械臂末端到达、四足 locomotion 等GPU加速机器人基准任务中，PPO-EAL在安全精度和奖励性能上均优于最先进的一阶安全RL基线；在接触丰富的齿轮装配任务中实现了零样本仿真到现实部署，显著提升任务成功率、降低峰值接触力并增强操作鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PPO-EAL是一个通用且可实际部署的安全强化学习框架，适用于多样化的安全关键型机器人系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising solution to accomplish complex robotic control tasks; however, most of the current work ignores the safety requirements. Safe RL seeks to maximize task performance while satisfying explicit physical constraints, but current algorithms struggle to learn the policy efficiently with precise constraint satisfaction. This work proposes PPO-EAL, a novel first-order constrained policy optimization framework that integrates exact augmented Lagrangian optimization into proximal policy optimization for safe robotic control. By combining clipped policy updates with exact quadratic penalty terms, PPO-EAL achieves theoretically grounded constraint enforcement without requiring impractically large penalty factors. A momentum-regulated multiplier update further improves dual-variable stability, reducing constraint oscillation and unsafe behavior while preserving task performance. We provide exactness and convergence analysis under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Extensive validation across diverse GPU-accelerated robotic benchmarks-including cart-pole balancing, cart-double-pendulum stabilization, 7-DoF Franka end-effector reaching, and quadrupedal locomotion-demonstrates superior safety precision and reward performance compared with state-of-the-art first-order safe RL baselines. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real deployment in a contact-rich gear assembly task, where PPO-EAL substantially improves task success, reduces peak contact force, and enhances operational robustness. These results establish PPO-EAL as a general and practically deployable safe RL framework for diverse safety-critical robotic systems.

</details>


### [63] [LocalNav: Distilling Frontier VLMs and Embodied RL for On-Device Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27871)
*Nicolas Baumann,Liam Boyle,Pu Deng,Edoardo Ghignone,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Luca Benini,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏策略，将大型前沿VLM的空间语义推理能力迁移至轻量级4B参数量本地VLM，并在嵌入式GPU（如Jetson Orin）上实现边缘部署；通过仅500条推理轨迹微调Qwen3.5-4B，在HM3D OVON基准上达到34.5%的成功率（大模型为39.7%）；引入E-RLVR与Token生成正则化，将Token开销降低72.1%、延迟降低71.8%，联合量化后累计推理延迟降低82.8%，为移动机器人本地低延迟VLM执行提供了可行范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型VLM在开放词汇物体导航（ObjectNav）等任务中表现优异，但其计算开销通常只能云端执行，难以在资源受限的机器人上实现低延迟本地部署。

Method: 1) 使用Claude Sonnet 4.6构建基于场景图（SG）的SOTA导航pipeline（SR 39.7%）；2) 仅用500条前沿推理轨迹对Qwen3.5-4B进行微调蒸馏；3) 提出E-RLVR（嵌入式强化学习与视觉推理）结合Token生成（TG）正则化，压缩输出序列长度；4) 联合量化技术进一步降低推理延迟。

Result: 蒸馏后的4B模型在HM3D OVON上达到34.5% SR（大模型39.7%）；E-RLVR+TG正则化减少72.1% Token开销和71.8%延迟；联合量化实现累计82.8%推理延迟降低，性能损失较小。

Conclusion: 所提蒸馏+压缩策略可有效在边缘GPU上实现低延迟VLM推理，为资源受限机器人部署复杂视觉语言导航能力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have emerged in the robotic domain as a powerful tool that enables environmental perception with language context, serving as a catalyst for open-vocabulary tasks like ObjectNav. Yet, their computational footprint typically confines them to cloud execution, hindering low-latency inference with local deployment on resource-constrained robots. To address this challenge, we present a distillation strategy that transfers complex spatial-semantic reasoning from large frontier models into a lightweight, 4B-parameter local VLM for edge execution on embedded GPU devices (e.g., Jetson Orin). We first establish a State of the Art (SotA), Scene Graph (SG)-based pipeline using Claude Sonnet 4.6, achieving a 39.7% Success Rate (SR) on the HM3D OVON benchmark. We then demonstrate that fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 500 frontier reasoning traces effectively enables navigation capabilities, yielding a SR of 34.5%, narrowing the gap to the performance of large cloud models. Finally, we introduce E-RLVR with Token Generation (TG) regularization to compress output sequence lengths for physical deployment while grounding the agent in its task. This downstream optimization reduces TG overhead by 72.1% and latency by 71.8%. Combined with quantization, this joint strategy yields a cumulative 82.8% reduction in overall inference latency without significantly sacrificing performance, presenting a viable paradigm for local, low-latency VLM execution on mobile robots.

</details>


### [64] [S$^2$-VLA: State-Space Guided Vision-Language-Action Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27872)
*Zhipeng Xie,Zongyi Han,Xiangyi Wei,Shiliang Sun,Yang Li,Jing Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S^2-VLA通过状态空间引导的自适应注意力机制（SSGAA），利用信念状态追踪任务进程并动态生成门控权重，自适应融合视觉、语言和动作三种特征，在2B参数规模下超越7B模型，在长序列机器人操作任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长序列任务中因静态特征融合机制导致累积误差传播，无法适应任务不同阶段的需求变化，性能严重下降。

Method: 提出SSGAA机制：维护一个信念状态来追踪任务进度，并据此生成动态门控权重，自适应融合视觉特征（空间感知）、任务意图（高层规划）和时间动作序列（执行一致性）三类互补信息。

Result: 仅2B参数规模的S^2-VLA在LIBERO和SimplerEnv等长序列操作基准上一致超越7B级大模型，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 自适应特征融合对于长序列机器人操作至关重要，信念状态引导的动态门控机制能有效缓解累积误差问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but their performance degrades significantly in long-horizon tasks due to cumulative error propagation. This limitation largely arises from static feature fusion mechanisms that rely on fixed weights to combine visual, language, and action representations, preventing the model from adapting to different phases of task execution. To address this limitation, we propose S$^2$-VLA, a framework that introduces a State-Space Guided Adaptive Attention (SSGAA) mechanism. SSGAA maintains a belief state that tracks task progression and generates dynamic gating weights to adaptively fuse information from three complementary sources visual features for spatial perception, task intents for high-level task planning, and temporal action sequences for execution consistency. This adaptive fusion allows the model to shift its focus throughout task execution, aligning with the evolving requirements of different task stages. Despite its compact 2B parameter size, S$^2$-VLA consistently outperforms larger 7B-scale models and achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and SimplerEnv. highlighting the importance of adaptive feature fusion for long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [65] [Swarm sign language: motion-based communication between drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27883)
*Thomas Rey,Julien Moras,Alexandre Eudes,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在隐身约束的群体机器人中，利用模块化、动态可行的平面轨迹作为视觉线索，通过运动进行非主动信息交换。接收端无人机使用位姿估计器追踪发送端姿态，并输入到自定义的3DTrajDecode解码器中进行时空序列分类与分割，同时回归其大小和法向量。通过可配置的在线程序化生成管道训练解码器，并在真实世界测试和仿真中验证了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐身约束的群体机器人中，主动射频传输易被干扰，需要一种替代的视觉通信方式进行非主动信息交换。

Method: 利用模块化、动态可行的平面轨迹作为视觉线索；接收端使用位姿估计器追踪发送端位姿，配合自定义3DTrajDecoder进行时空序列分类、分割及大小和法向量回归；通过可配置的在线程序化生成管道进行鲁棒训练。

Result: 通过真实世界测试和仿真验证了系统运行域，并通过消融研究详细说明了架构选择和系统局限性。

Conclusion: 运动基视觉通信在隐身约束的群体机器人中是一种可行且有效的非主动信息交换方式。

Abstract: In stealth-constrained swarm robotics, visual communication provides a critical alternative to active radio transmissions, which might be jammed. This research investigates motion-based communication for non-active information exchange, utilizing modular, dynamically feasible planar trajectories as visual cues. On the receiver drone end, a pose estimator tracks the transmitting drone's pose, feeding it into our custom 3DTrajDecoder. The decoder is designed to classify and segment the spatiotemporal sequence while simultaneously regressing its size and normal vector. To robustly train the decoder on both communicative and non-communicative trajectories, we developed a configurable online procedural generation pipeline. We validate our system through real-world testing and simulation to define its operating domain, supported by an extensive ablation study detailing our architectural choices and system limitations.

</details>


### [66] [When Multi-Robot Systems Meet Agentic AI:Towards Embodied Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27929)
*Yuxuan Yan,Yuanyuan Jia,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身集体智能"(ECI)概念，探讨未来多机器人系统如何超越共享地图和任务分配，转向通过共感知、共行动、共进化三个层面共享具身智能体循环产生的世界上下文、任务进度和技能经验。通过导航实验验证了共享世界记忆继承的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI向智能体化演进、机器人研究从单机自主走向多机系统，机器人团队需要超越传统的地图、任务分配和数据共享，实现具身智能体循环所产生状态的共享，以应对更广泛感知、分布式行动、异构能力和容错需求。

Method: 1) 综述具身AI向智能体化发展和多机器人协作的演进；2) 提出具身集体智能框架，包含共感知、共行动、共进化三个维度；3) 通过导航实验验证共享世界记忆继承这一具体组件。

Result: 导航研究表明，新加入的机器人可以从合并后的团队记忆中获益，验证了共享世界记忆继承的概念可行，但该实验并非对ECI框架的完整评估。

Conclusion: 具身集体智能(ECI)为具身多智能体智能提供了一个有前景的研究方向，案例研究为概念中可测量的部分提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Embodied AI is increasingly becoming agentic, shifting robots from perception--control pipelines towards closed-loop systems that can retrieve context, deliberate during execution, monitor feedback, and refine future behavior. In parallel, robotics research has also moved from single-robot autonomy towards multi-robot systems, driven by the need for wider sensing, distributed action, heterogeneous capabilities, and fault tolerance. As AI agents move from single-agent use towards multi-agent collaboration, robotics faces a parallel challenge: robot teams must move beyond sharing maps, task assignments, and datasets towards sharing the state produced by embodied agent loops. This article explores Embodied Collective Intelligence (ECI), a future multi-robot paradigm in which a robot team accumulates and uses world context, task progress, and skill experience as shared resources. Specifically, we first review how embodied AI is becoming agentic and how multi-robot cooperation has evolved. We then present Embodied Collective Intelligence through Co-Perception, Co-Action, and Co-Evolution. Finally, we use an illustrative navigation study to examine one concrete component of the concept: shared world-memory inheritance. The study shows that a newly added robot can benefit from merged team memory, but it is not intended as a full evaluation of the ECI framework. Taken together, the review and conceptual framework motivate Embodied Collective Intelligence as a direction for embodied multi-agent intelligence, while the case study grounds one measurable part of the concept.

</details>


### [67] [Building a Scalable, Reproducible, Evaluatable, and Closed-Loop Simulation Environment Foundation for Embodied Intelligence Cloud-Native Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied Intelligence Training, Evaluation, and Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27962)
*Junwu Xiong,Yongjian Guo,Mingxi Luo,Ning Qiao,Lei Kang,Song Wang,Yince Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向具身智能的云原生仿真基础设施框架，通过弹性资源调度、容器化仿真、统一数据管理和面向服务设计，支持大规模训练、标准化评估和仿真数据收集，构建了可扩展、可复现的四层架构平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决真实机器人数据收集成本高、可扩展性有限、可复现性差的问题，为具身智能提供统一的仿真基础设施。

Method: 采用云原生技术（弹性资源调度、容器化仿真、统一数据管理、面向服务设计），构建四层架构，集成D-VLA、RL-VLA3、Sword、Pre-VLA等系统，实现仿真环境生成、任务执行、轨迹收集、模型评估、数据管理和云服务的一体化平台。

Result: 实现了可扩展的多模型多任务大规模仿真框架，支持标准化评估、仿真数据采集和闭环数据优化。

Conclusion: 云原生仿真基础设施能够为数据生成、模型训练、标准化评估和真实部署提供统一基础，将在具身智能未来发展中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: This paper presents a cloud-native simulation infrastructure framework for embodied intelligence that supports large-scale training, standardized evaluation, and simulation-based data collection. The framework unifies simulation environment generation, task execution, trajectory collection, model evaluation, data management, and cloud services into a scalable and reproducible platform.
  To address the high cost, limited scalability, and poor reproducibility of real-world robotic data collection, the framework adopts cloud-native technologies including elastic resource scheduling, containerized simulation, unified data management, and service-oriented system design, enabling efficient large-scale simulation for multi-model and multi-task workloads.
  Built on a four-layer architecture, the framework provides standardized environment assets, automated task generation, trajectory collection, benchmark evaluation, and closed-loop data optimization. It further integrates representative systems including D-VLA, RL-VLA3, Sword, and Pre-VLA to support scalable simulation, dynamic scheduling, visual augmentation, and real-time data filtering.
  We argue that cloud-native simulation infrastructure provides a unified foundation for data generation, model training, standardized evaluation, and real-world deployment, and will play a key role in the future development of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [68] [Translation as a Bridging Action: Transferring Manipulation Skills from Humans to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28133)
*Sijin Chen,Kaixuan Jiang,Haixin Shi,Yanhui Wang,Weiheng Zhong,Haosheng Li,Bo Jiang,Yuxiao Liu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种桥接动作表示（相对腕部平移），用于将人类双手操作技能迁移到双臂机器人上，比使用嘈杂的6DoF人体动作更有效，且随数据量扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类动作数据廉价、丰富且多样，是扩展机器人学习的宝贵资源，但直接将人体动作迁移到机器人存在手部姿态估计噪声大、人类手指与平行夹爪接触模式差异大等问题，学习包含旋转的人体动作信号是次优的。

Method: 提出桥接动作表示：在初始头部相机帧中的相对腕部平移（旋转无关），该动作空间为人类和机器人共享；并构建类π₀的视觉-语言-动作模型，使用交错动作令牌和注意力掩码来处理不同本体中可能缺失的动作组件。

Result: 在一套新颖的双手操作任务上，所提出的桥接动作在将人类操作知识迁移到机器人上远优于嘈杂的6DoF人体动作，并且随人类数据量增加而扩展。

Conclusion: 旋转无关的相对腕部平移作为桥接动作表示，能有效实现从人类到双臂机器人的操作技能迁移，且具有数据扩展性。

Abstract: We study whether we can learn novel manipulation skills from human actions to a bi-manual robot with parallel grippers. Human action data is cheap, abundant, and diverse, making it one of the most promising resources for scaling up robot learning. Yet transferring skills from humans to robots remains hard: most prior work treats humans as just another bi-manual 6DoF embodiment, where hand-pose estimates are noisy and the contact patterns of human fingers differ fundamentally from those of a parallel gripper. We argue that learning rotation-inclusive action signals from human data is therefore sub-optimal, and instead propose a bridging action representation: the relative wrist translation within the initial head-camera frame, an action space shared by humans and robots. To handle the potential absence of certain action components in different embodiments, we build a $π_0$-like vision-language-action model with interleaved action tokens and attention masking. On a suite of novel bi-manual manipulation tasks, our bridging action transfers human manipulation knowledge to robots far more effectively than noisy 6DoF human actions and scales with the amount of human data.

</details>


### [69] [PA-BiCoop: A Primary-Auxiliary Cooperative Framework for General Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28192)
*Bai Qicheng,Wang Ziru,Ma Teli,Dai Guang,Wang Jingdong,Wang Mengmeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-BiCoop，一种基于主-辅助臂动态分工的双臂协作框架，通过共享全局特征编码器和两个专用解码器实现双臂协调操作，在仿真和真实任务中分别比现有方法平均提升48%和50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作方法要么缺乏双臂间交互，要么忽视动态分工需求，将双臂视为功能等价，无法充分利用双臂协作的灵活性和效率优势。

Method: PA-BiCoop框架将机械臂分为主臂和辅助臂，角色可自适应调整；采用共享全局特征编码器搭配两个专用解码器：主解码器生成主臂的基座标位姿和核心任务affordance热图，辅助解码器输出辅助臂在主臂坐标系下的相对位姿；并设计动态角色分配模块自动映射左右臂角色。

Result: 在RLBench2仿真任务中平均优于现有方法48%，在真实世界任务中平均提升超过50%。

Conclusion: PA-BiCoop通过动态主-辅助臂分工机制有效提升了双臂协作性能，验证了该框架在双臂操作中的有效性和先进性。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation is essential for advanced robotic systems because it offers higher efficiency and flexibility compared to single-arm configurations. However, existing approaches either lack inter-arm interaction or ignore the need for a dynamic division of labor, treating the arms as functionally equivalent. To address these limitations, this paper draws inspiration from human bimanual manipulation where one arm handles core operations and the other provides auxiliary support, and proposes PA-BiCoop, a new single-model bimanual cooperation framework with dynamic primary-auxiliary arm differentiation. PA-BiCoop categorizes robotic arms into primary and auxiliary arms with adaptively adjustable roles across task stages, employs two specialized decoders that share a global feature encoder: the primary decoder generates the primary arm's base-coordinate pose and core-task affordance heatmaps, and the auxiliary decoder outputs the auxiliary arm's relative pose in the primary arm's coordinate system. Moreover, we design a dynamic role assignment module to automatically map roles to left/right arms without manual pre-definition. This design facilitates inter-arm knowledge sharing and coordinated manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PA-BiCoop achieves superior performance: it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 48% on average in RLBench2 simulation tasks and by over 50% on average in real world tasks, thereby verifying its effectiveness and advancement in bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [70] [Learning Stable In-Grasp Manipulation in a Non-Dropping Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28196)
*Ha Thang Long Doan,Hikaru Arita,Kazuto Nakashima,Kenji Tahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过将灵巧操作技能分解为多个可分析的组件，并利用经典物理和控制理论约束来指导各组件的学习，实现了高效且稳定的灵巧操作技能学习，在抓取稳定性和重定位/重定向方面优于传统方法和纯强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的灵巧操作控制器基于分析模型，受限于强假设；而强化学习方法虽然端到端探索，但因不稳定性和学习目标冲突而效率低下。本文旨在通过理论指导的分解式学习来高效探索稳定且准确的操作技能。

Method: 将灵巧操作技能分解为多个简单/可分析的组件，每个技能组件在经典物理和控制理论的约束与指导下进行学习。

Result: 在稳定抓取、抓取内重定位/重定向、不同物体、传感器/电机噪声、延迟和摩擦条件等场景下，加入理论先验知识的技能学习变得高效且稳定。

Conclusion: 融合经典物理和控制理论先验知识可以显著提升灵巧操作技能学习的效率和稳定性。

Abstract: Traditionally, dexterous manipulation controllers are designed using analytic models constrained by strong assumptions about the hand and the objects being manipulated. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become another common approach in which skills are explored openly in an end-to-end manner but is inefficient because of unnoticeable instability and conflicts in learning objectives. This paper attempts to efficiently explore stable and accurate manipulation skills by decomposing dexterous skills into multiple simpler/analyzable components. Each skill component is subsequently learned with constraints and guidance from classical physics and control theory. Our work shows that for stable grasp, in-grasp reposition/reorientation with different objects, sensor/motor noise, latency, and frictional conditions, skill learning becomes efficient and stable with prior knowledge from theory.

</details>


### [71] [Unleashing Infinite Motion: Scaling Expressive Quadrupedal Motion via Generative Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28237)
*Youzhi Liu,Li Gao,Yifei Qian,Liu Liu,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-Mo 提出了一种全自动流水线，通过 LLM 生成运动提示、视频扩散模型合成机器人行为、将生成视频提升为 3D 参考轨迹来训练跟踪策略，从而无需动物数据即可为四足机器人生成多样化运动。在 Unitree Go2 上实现 96.7% 的真实部署成功率，并开源了包含 7,488 个语言标注运动的数据集 Quad-Imaginarium。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人运动局限于少数步态，缺乏丰富的行为表达。传统方法依赖动物运动数据（如狗），但数据采集依赖动物配合、跨物种重建脆弱、不同形态间重定向不适定。作者希望消除对动物数据的依赖，将数据稀缺问题重构为生成问题。

Method: 1) LLM 提出运动提示；2) 视频扩散模型根据提示合成机器人行为视频；3) 引入身份一致性损失（Identity Consistency Loss）确保生成视频帧间外观一致性，从而可靠提取 3D 参考轨迹；4) 将 3D 轨迹用于训练跟踪策略，部署到真实 Unitree Go2 机器人上。

Result: 生成了包含 7,488 个语言标注四足运动的数据集（约 18.5 小时），涵盖杂技和表演性行为。在真实 Unitree Go2 上随机测试 392 个运动，部署成功率为 96.7%；全数据集在仿真中成功率为 97.6%。

Conclusion: Uni-Mo 成功实现了无需动物数据的四足机器人多样化运动生成，展示了从生成视频到真实机器人策略训练的全自动可行路径，为机器人行为丰富化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have achieved remarkable locomotion, yet their behavioral repertoire remains confined to a few gaits--far from the expressive, companion-like presence long envisioned for them. Attempts to import the humanoid recipe of large-scale motion data have inherited one tacit assumption: that robot motion must first pass through an animal body, making data collection dependent on cooperative animals, reconstruction fragile across species, and retargeting ill-posed across incompatible morphologies. We propose Uni-Mo, a fully automated pipeline that removes the animal from the loop by reframing data scarcity as a generation problem: an LLM proposes motion prompts, a video diffusion model synthesizes the corresponding robot behaviors, and the generated videos are lifted into 3D reference trajectories used to train tracking policies deployed on a real Unitree Go2. To make naively-drifting generations reliably extractable, we introduce an Identity Consistency Loss that enforces appearance coherence across frames. We release Quad-Imaginarium at https://github.com/GaoLii/Quad-Imaginarium.git, the resulting open-source dataset of 7,488 language-annotated quadruped motions (18.5 hours) spanning acrobatic and performative behaviors. We validate 392 randomly sampled motions on a real Unitree Go2 with a 96.7% deployment success rate, complemented by a 97.6% success rate across the full dataset in simulation.

</details>


### [72] [SimFoundry: Modular and Automated Scene Generation for Policy Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28276)
*Nadun Ranawaka,Josiah Wong,Wei-Lin Pai,Wei-Teng Chu,Tianyuan Dai,Masoud Moghani,Hang Yin,Yunfan Jiang,Wesley Durbano,Brandon Huynh,Yu Fang,Linxi Fan,Danfei Xu,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Bowen Wen,Ajay Mandlekar,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimFoundry是一个模块化、自动化的系统，能够从视频零样本构建真实到仿真的场景，生成支持编辑的数字孪生及其变体（数字表亲），用于训练可在真实世界中零样本迁移的策略，在7个操作任务和5种策略架构上，仿真评估与真实世界性能的皮尔逊相关系数均值达0.911，且引入对象、场景、任务变体后真实世界任务成功率分别提升17%、21%、40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界中训练和评估机器人策略成本高、难以规模化扩展，因此需要一种高效、可自动化的方法来生成仿真环境，以便在仿真中训练策略并直接迁移到真实世界。

Method: SimFoundry——从视频零样本自动构建真实到仿真场景的系统，生成支持编辑的数字孪生，并自动生成保持操作属性的多样化数字表亲（场景、对象、任务的变体）。

Result: 在7个操作任务和5种策略架构上，仿真评估与真实世界性能的皮尔逊相关系数均值为0.911，最大排名违规均值为0.018；训练时引入对象、场景、任务数字表亲，真实世界零样本策略任务成功率分别提升17%、21%、40%。

Conclusion: SimFoundry能够高效生成仿真训练数据，策略可零样本迁移到复杂的真实操作任务，且数字表亲增强了策略对新真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Training and evaluating robot policies in the real world is costly and difficult to scale. We introduce SimFoundry, a modular and automated system for zero-shot real-to-sim scene construction from a video. SimFoundry generates sim-ready digital twins and supports object, scene, and task editing, enabling the automated generation of diverse digital cousins: affordance-preserving variations of reconstructed real-world scenes.
